- **2026-11-04 — infinite primitive irreducible non-unit Pisot family has simultaneous level-three strong coincidence, avoiding a redundant single-specimen seam argument.** For every integer k>=1, sigma_k=(01, 1^k 2 1^k, 012) has M=[[1,0,1],[1,2k,1],[0,1,1]], det=2k, f_k(x)=x²(x-2)-2k(x-1)² irreducible (gcd argument for all integer roots), discriminant -4k(8k²-13k+16)<0 and Perron root >2k, hence PIP. All sigma_k³(a), a=0,1,2, have *the same* Parikh prefix (1,2k²,k) at index p=k(2k+1)+1 and next letter 1. Thus **every** noncoincident irreducible balanced pair produces an actual diagonal (1,1) child within three full generations, and no finite strictly child-closed noncoincident component can exist for any member. The k=1 specimen is exactly 01/121/012 of draft PR #475; its existing local seam parity remains a separate conditional filter but is not required for this specimen's exclusion, because all three letter pairs are already strongly coincident by level 3. This is a *family theorem*, not general C4/PSC/PDS or a proof that strong coincidence alone implies pure discrete spectrum. Source: [uniform third-level family proof](c4-uniform-third-level-coincidence-family-2026-11-04.md), canonical Mojo and independent Python literal-oracle controls.

- **2026-11-04 — symmetric run equality is unnecessary for the non-unit PIP strong-coincidence family.** For *all integers* a,b>=1, the asymmetric substitution `sigma=(01,1^a 2 1^b,012)` has an incidence matrix depending only on s=a+b, with det=s, `chi=x²(x−2)−s(x−1)²` irreducible, primitive and Pisot. All three actual `sigma³(letter)` have common prefix Parikh `(1,a(a+b),a)` at index `p=a(a+b+1)+1` followed by letter 1. Therefore **every nonempty irreducible noncoincident balanced pair has a genuine diagonal (1,1) factor in its complete third-generation inflation**, uniformly for the two-parameter infinite family; no fully strict noncoincident child-closed component is possible. For a fixed s all s−1 positive run splittings share the entire incidence/Perron data yet their *explicit* common word address varies with a (at s=4: p=6,11,16); do **not** infer occurrence addresses from incidence alone or assert earliest coincidence. This extends merged #476, not general C4/PDS/PSC. Source: [asymmetric family argument](c4-asymmetric-third-level-coincidence-2026-11-04.md), same canonical Mojo implementation and independent literal Python oracle.

- **2026-11-04 — fully close both one-sided zero-run boundaries of the asymmetric Pisot coincidence family.** The two-parameter `(01,1^a 2 1^b,012)` family is primitive irreducible Pisot for **every a,b>=0, a+b=s>=1**, with det(M)=s; it is unimodular only for s=1. All three letter images have a simultaneous equal-Parikh prefix followed by the same letter by **level at most four**, with exact cases: `a=0,b>=1`: level 2, p=0, Q=(0,0,0), letter 0; `a,b>=1`: level 3, p=a(s+1)+1, Q=(1,as,a), letter 1 (merged #477); `a>=1,b=0`: **new level 4** at p=a³+a²+4a+1, Q=(a+1,a³+2a,a(a+1)), letter 1. The right-boundary proof uses actual second-level blocks F=AB,G=B^aC,H=ABC and both literal fourth images; it does not apply the b>0 formula outside its hypotheses. Thus **every** irreducible noncoincident balanced pair in this closed quadrant has a literal diagonal (z,z) child by generation four, excluding fully strict closed components **for this family only**. For fixed s=4, the five splittings exhibit distinct witness (level,position) pairs (2,0),(3,6),(3,11),(3,16),(4,97) despite identical incidence/Perron data; none is asserted earliest. The singular a=b=0 input is excluded. Scope: [closed-run boundary family proof](c4-closed-run-family-four-level-coincidence-2026-11-04.md), canonical `c4_third_level_coincidence_family.mojo` extension and independent Python oracle. No general C4/G1/BH/PDS/PSC claim promotion.

- **2026-11-04 — strict C4 productivity is strong coincidence on the cut pairs; a strict system forces a positive-measure overlap of the corresponding Rauzy subtiles.** For a supplied strict system C the unordered first-letter pairs A(C) label every balanced cut of every inflation, are closed and never collapsed under the initial-letter map g×g, and contain no strongly coincident pair (Theorem A). Under irreducible Pisot hypotheses a cut-point lattice count gives `λ(⋃_{A(C)} R_c∩R_c') ≥ κ/L_max > 0` (Theorem B). In the unit case this sits strictly between pairwise strong coincidence (Arnoux–Ito import) and C4, so pairwise measure-disjointness of the cut-pair subtiles would suffice. Unconditional disjointness appears open; adelic non-unit form not proved. Reducible strict control `10/102/211` has |A(C)|=1 (`{1,2}`), so a single cut pair is not excluded combinatorially. Locator: [C4 cut-pair productivity](c4-cut-pair-productivity-2026-11-04.md), `kernel/psc/c4_cut_pair_reduction.mojo`, issue #461.

- **2026-11-04 — correction to the preceding cut-pair/Rauzy-subtile entry (no theorem promotion):** the precise proved finite combinatorics are **(i)** every *proper* balanced cut in a supplied fully strict closed system has a first-letter pair in its nonempty set A(C), **(ii)** A(C) is noncollapsing under the first-letter map, **(iii)** no pair in A(C) has a strong-coincidence witness, and **(iv)** a nonproductive *finite-reachable* seed yields a strict sink. These are NOT an equivalence between a missing witness on one arbitrarily chosen letter pair and nonproductivity. Crucially, Arnoux–Ito Corollary 2 obtains zero overlap for **primed** subtiles from one positive strong-coincidence pair; unprimed subtiles become pairwise measure-disjoint in their Theorem 2 under **strong coincidence for all pairs**, not by applying a single-pair claim to unprimed R_c. The previous assertion that the cut-pair unprimed measure result is "strictly between pairwise SC and C4" was unsupported and is **withdrawn**. The quantitative compact-window lattice-density argument is a separate proposed conditional measure reduction, still requiring independent review of the regular-box model-set count/covolume normalization; neither the finite Mojo audit nor a bounded missing-SC witness verifies it. Locator: [corrected C4 cut-pair argument, sections 4 and 7](c4-cut-pair-productivity-2026-11-04.md), Arnoux–Ito 2001 Theorem 2 and Corollary 2, Issue #461.

- **2026-11-04 — a strict C4 system halves lattice occupancy.** The top and bottom `c`-vertices of any inflation of a strict state are disjoint lattice points over `R_c`, by Theorem A(1). Hence the occupancy `θ_c = κ f_c/(ℓ̄ λ(R_c))` satisfies `θ_c ≤ 1/2` for every letter, with no unimodularity and no Arnoux–Ito import. It depends only on the §8 regular-box count. Contrapositive: one letter with occupancy above one half excludes C4. Classical double-covering mechanism; the identification `θ = 1/m` with a covering degree is not proved. Locator: [C4 cut-pair productivity §3a](c4-cut-pair-productivity-2026-11-04.md), issue #461.

- **2026-11-04 — every represented first-letter cut pair in a supplied fully strict SCC has positive Perron frequency on a correct cyclic subsequence.** The exact complete child incidence N satisfies L^T N=beta L^T. For each unordered first pair e the h cyclic classes of N give delta_e=h max_k(sum of right-PF weights of states with e in class k)/(L·r)>0; choose a source j in that class and n divisible by h to obtain a literal balanced-cut occurrence ratio tending to delta_e. Under the fixed-origin regular-window and uniform subtile-approximation inputs of merged #480, **each** represented pair has a proposed positive unprimed Rauzy-overlap lower bound kappa delta_e, not merely a positive overlap for some pair in the union; independent geometric audit remains pending. The strict non-Pisot three-rotation control has ratio 1/9 for every pair; an imprimitive synthetic two-class matrix shows why arbitrary n is insufficient. No arbitrary-PIP C4/G1/BH/A1'/PSC promotion. Locator: [C4 per-pair PF frequency](c4-every-cut-pair-positive-density-2026-11-04.md), issue #461 and merged #480.

- **2026-11-04 — correction to the "correction to the preceding cut-pair/Rauzy-subtile entry" (Arnoux–Ito labels).** The positive-prefix `R_c` is Arnoux–Ito's **primed** family up to sign (`X(c)' = −R_c`, `X(c) = −(R_c+πe_c)`; Def. 6 with Lemma 1, read in the published text). So one left strong-coincidence pair gives `λ(R_c∩R_c') = 0` for unimodular PIP σ (proof of their Thm 2, first sentence; self-contained Lemma P). The withdrawal of that one-pair statement was a labelling error and is reversed **for the unit case only**. The chain SC(A(C)) ⇒ disjoint(A(C)) ⇒ no strict C holds but is no stronger than Theorem A(4); "strictly between" stays withdrawn; non-unit and suffix-to-prefix transfers are not claimed. Locator: [cut-pair note §4](c4-cut-pair-productivity-2026-11-04.md), [literature gate](c4-prefix-subtile-convention-literature-gate-2026-11-04.md).

- **2026-11-04 — occupancy route retired as a C4 attack (unit case).** Lemma P's equality step gives `λ(R_c) = s f_c`, so Theorem C's occupancy is letter-independent. Under labelled imports (self-replicating multiple tiling of degree `p`; tiling ⇔ PDS), `θ = 1/p` and `θ > 1/2 ⇔ PDS`, so Theorem C's contrapositive is "PDS ⇒ no strict system", already given by Theorem A(4) with Akiyama–Lee. Non-unit Euclidean occupancy is not an exclusion tool either: an exact residue-refined computation reported by the 2026-11-04 audit workflow gives occupancy below one half on every letter of PIP `1/021/001` (det 2), every pair of which is left-SC. That computation is an **unported scratch prototype**, not repository evidence, until it is ported to `kernel/`. Locator: [cut-pair note §3a](c4-cut-pair-productivity-2026-11-04.md).

- **2026-11-04 — GIFS comparison retired at the measure level.** For a strict system, the cut-point sets `K(G)` (matrix `N^T`, contraction `M_s`) embed in the subtiles (matrix `M^T`) through both tracks, with an integer multiplicity `m` on each `R_c`. The resulting measure identities are consistent for every `m ≥ 2`. A contradiction would need `λ(R) < 2κ/ℓ̄`, which has PDS strength in the unit case. The "next concrete step" proposed in #480 §6 therefore does not reach beyond PDS. Locator: [cut-pair note §6](c4-cut-pair-productivity-2026-11-04.md).

Fixed-coset regular-window counts prove the lifted strict cut-pair lower bound `mu(Z_A) >= kappa/L_max` and the two-track half-occupancy condition. - **2026-11-04 — each phase of any fully strict irreducible ternary Pisot balanced-pair SCC has its OWN full Parikh rank three.** In a supplied occurrence-complete strict SCC, P N=M P. If N has graph period h and phase classes C_k, then P_k (N^h)|C_k=M^h P_k. Every beta^h is an irreducible cubic Perron eigenvalue (the conjugates of Pisot beta contract), so each nonzero invariant phase image P_k has rank 3. Thus every phase contains at least three actual states and |C|>=3h. The phase-restricted right-Perron vectors also satisfy P v^(k)=P v/h for each k: exact Perron-weighted original-letter Parikh equipartition. Any 3–5-state strict PIP SCC must be primitive; a hypothetical period-two strict component needs at least six actual states. Synthetic integral block-periodic matrices with 3h states saturate only the algebraic bound, not word-level strict-realizability; the genuine reducible strict four-state control fails the rank test. Source: [periodic phase Parikh rigidity](c4-periodic-parikh-phase-rigidity-2026-11-04.md); issue #461. No general C4/G1/BH/A1'/PDS/PSC promotion, and independent/hosted acceptance remains pending.

(iii) The required literature stop/go gate was filed late, at review. - **2026-11-04 — sharpen the forward two-run Pisot family to the exact *minimum simultaneous* left-coincidence generation.** Uniform in all a,b>=0 with a+b>=1, the minimum three-source equal-Parikh/common-next-letter level is 2 for a=0 or a=1,b>=1; 3 for a=1,b=0 or a>=2,b>=1; and 4 for a>=2,b=0. The new a=1,b>=1 generation-two witness is p=b+3, Q=(1,b+1,1), next 1; the a=1,b=0 generation-three witness is p=7, Q=(2,3,2), next 1. Explicit zero-count and two-count prefix separation excludes levels 1 and 2 as applicable and all third-generation candidates for the a>=2,b=0 ray (balanced positions 0, a(a+1)+2, a(a+1)+a+3 have mismatching next letters). This CORRECTS the *nonminimal interpretation* of the previously exhibited #479 witness levels, without invalidating those witnesses. Full incidence is fixed at a+b=s while even the exact minimum generation varies with the split. Canonical Mojo has a separate sharp-depth function so older receipts remain reproducible, independent Python scans every earlier candidate cut, complete swap diagonals checked. - **2026-11-04 — literal prefix-satellite lifting and sharp all-pair strong coincidence in the forward family (extension to PR #483).** The general word lemma: if a substitution has literal τ(k)=τ(i)W, then τⁿ(k)=τⁿ(i)τⁿ⁻¹(W), so *every* (i,j) equal-Parikh-prefix/next-letter witness is simultaneously an (i,j,k) witness, and the minimum depths agree. Here σ(2)=σ(0)2; a second *family-specific* calculation proves pair {1,2} has exactly the same minimum depth D(a,b) as {0,1}, while {0,2} has depth 1. At level 2 for a>=2, B^a C B^b has only one zero while ABC already has two before its first zero; at level 3 for a>=2,b=0, the entire {1,2} longer-tail cut comparison is excluded by exact prefix counts (including separate a=2,3 controls), not merely by the {0,1} prefix argument. Hence the earliest **all-letter-pairs** strong-coincidence generation is the five-chamber D, and this sharp horizon varies with fixed incidence. Python adds exhaustive pairwise earlier-level scans and a same-incidence word-order negative control. This is not a general C4/G1/BH/PDS/PSC or arbitrary-root first-productivity result; source: [sharp-depth proof, prefix-satellite extension](c4-forward-run-family-sharp-depth-2026-11-04.md).

- **2026-11-04 — exact prime-supported lattice transport in every cyclic phase of a hypothetical strict ternary Pisot SCC.** Given complete literal PN=MP, phasewise rational rank three and actual period h, define L_k as the Z-lattice generated by all Parikh columns of phase k and g_k=[Z³:L_k] (gcd of its 3x3 minors). Then M L_k ⊆ L_(k+1), the integer transition indices t_k=|det M|g_k/g_(k+1) satisfy ∏t_k=|det M|^h and can have no prime factors outside det M. At primes p∤det M the p-primary Smith factors remain unchanged across phases. For a unit substitution, lattice transport is exact equality and every g_k is identical; for equality-size |C|=3h, every 3x3 literal transition A_k is nonsingular with |det A_k|=t_k. Synthetic period-two determinant-two systems attain (t0,t1)=(1,4),(2,2),(4,1), including all-positive Parikh columns, but are NOT literal strict balanced-pair realizations. Source: [C4 phase-lattice index conservation](c4-phase-lattice-index-conservation-2026-11-04.md), stacked on draft #484, Issue #461. - **2026-11-06 — Proposition Q: a finite child-closed balanced-pair system has a neutral quotient spectrum.** For any primitive substitution on any alphabet, the Parikh span `V` of a finite child-closed system is `M`-invariant, and every root of `χ_M` on `ℚ^d/V` is 0 or a root of unity (bounded gaps kill expanding quotient directions; Kronecker does the rest). Hence `rank P = d` whenever `M` has no neutral eigenvalue, PR #460's conclusion without Pisot. For PIP the quotient is trivial, so this adds nothing to C4; it explains why every known strict system lives on a substitution with a neutral eigenvalue. Pinned on one strict system of each of the 28 strict-carrier classes with images of length ≤ 3 (ranks 10/17/1) with an independent oracle. Locator: [general-structure note §2](c4-general-structure-2026-11-06.md), `kernel/psc/c4_quotient_spectrum.mojo`.

- **2026-11-06 — Theorems R2 and R3: the defect spectrum decides part of the Pisot-free strengthening.** For an irreducible cubic, a strict system with some `K₂ ≠ 0` forces Pisot (R2), and if both non-Perron roots lie outside the unit disc there is no strict system at all (R3), by the lowest-degree intertwiner, `ρ(N) = β` and Galois transitivity. So "irreducible `χ_M` ⇒ no strict system" is equivalent to C4 for all PIP together with the totally real one-root-outside class. This does not touch PIP: every spectral identity in use is satisfiable there. Locator: [general-structure note §3](c4-general-structure-2026-11-06.md).

- **2026-11-06 — negative controls for general C4.** (i) `01/2/120` (`χ_M = x²(x−2)`) carries a 20-state strict system with `rank P = 3`: full Parikh rank is not an obstruction ("rank P ≤ 2" refuted; pinned). (ii) A Tribonacci (M, N, P, D) packet satisfies every degree-two identity in use (`PN = MP`, `DN = WD`, `χ_N = χ_M χ_W`, `L > 0`, `Dz = 0`) but some of its columns are not the defect of any irreducible balanced pair with that Parikh vector (Parikh `(2,0,1)` admits only `K₂ = (0,±2,0)`; binary irreducible pairs of length `n` have `|K_ab| ≥ n−1`): algebra alone cannot close degree two, and per-column realisability is the next unused constraint. (iii) The A7 branching sketch was incomplete; its repair A7′ (conditional, unit PIP) gives `cr ≥ 3` only from a one-track branching over an unwitnessed hub pair, which nothing forces (the reducible control branches only over its good edge and carries 12 strict triples at `Φ²`, scratch). Locator: [general-structure note §4](c4-general-structure-2026-11-06.md).

Not repository evidence until ported; the 28 class representatives are pinned natively. Locator: [general-structure note §6](c4-general-structure-2026-11-06.md).

- **2026-11-06 — Correction to the prior-art-audit entry above, and the outcome of the Corollary B check (`wf_7fa1fcb8-25a`).** (a) That entry said "Proof gaps were fixed: R1(c) `D₂(ker P) = ℚ³`". The first version's proof of R1(c) was already complete (the image of `(P, D₂)` is `(M ⊕ W)`-invariant, and `χ_W(M ⊕ W)` with `χ_W(M)` invertible gives `ℚ³ ⊕ 0`); it was rewritten, not repaired. R1(c)'s size and divisibility conclusions are coprimality corollaries of prior statements, so only the joint `D₂(ker P) = ℚ³` formulation is new. (b) The proof lens confirmed R2 and R3 without strictness, Corollary B and R3′, with one terminology gap: "noncoincident" must mean `u ≠ v`, since the reducible `(01,01)` has a finite closure; it is now defined. Exact scratch controls: swap-seeded closures of five irreducible non-Pisot specimens reached a 10⁶-letter budget without closing (consistency only); `ex:nonpisot` closes with 4 states, all with `K₂ ≠ 0`. (c) The literature lens found no earlier proof in the sources it could read. Unread: Livshits 1987/1992, Hollander's thesis, the body of Hollander–Solomyak 2003, Solomyak 1992, Barge–Kwapisz 2006. It refuted the non-claim about prefix seeds in the one-outside class with an elementary discrepancy argument. If `bpa-W` terminates, every length-`|ζⁿW|` factor has Parikh vector within `ℓ` of `Mⁿw`, so `‖Mⁿw − |ζⁿW| f‖ ≤ ℓ`, and all conjugates `|λ| ≤ 1`. That is now Proposition B′ (any alphabet: Pisot or Salem; three letters: Pisot). It was re-derived by the author and is labelled literature-facing, plausibly folklore. (d) The attribution lens corrected locators (W₃ §4, cut-attractor §7, certificate §§3, 5), the `thm:sieve` range (`r ≥ 4`), the scope of Q1's prior proofs, the three-letter condition of Q1, open problems versus NS, and the Lean `Psc.target1` scope; and it removed "proved" from Corollary B while its check was pending. Locator: `tests/test_recorded_data_integrity.py`, [original research workflow](https://github.com/larsbx/pisot-substitution-conjecture-research/actions/runs/42014453294).
- **2026-11-08 — R1 support checker decidability is needed again at proof recovery.** A locally supplied `@decide` instance does not register finite decidability for later `of_decide_eq_true` calls through ordinary proposition definitions. At PR #520 head `aa8951221f4d3f80336efc9dc104e06018ba32d0`, hosted support compilation reached Lean and failed at three such calls, then the audit rejected `sorryAx`; the independent Quint pass did not establish Lean acceptance. Shared computable instances repair this boundary without changing strict-support, rank-progress or source-completeness propositions, including the downstream bounded witness. Locator: [diagnosis, exact-head acceptance contract and scope](psc-r1-decidability-repair-2026-11-08.md).

- **2026-11-08 — R1 executable validation and transitive proof axioms are separate evidence.** First repair candidate `4636ecb78bd1e650c9b895b0ed094ccc97303f72` compiled all 47 existing theorems and executed fourteen Boolean checks, but the additional checker-only axiom budget rejected a transitive `Classical.choice` dependency in the inferred finite decision instance. That dependency is not proof of runtime noncomputability. Scoped priority for core `Nat.decidableForallFin` / `Nat.decidableExistsFin` avoids generic Fintype proof transports while preserving all propositions and the stricter budget. Locator: [repair acceptance note](psc-r1-decidability-repair-2026-11-08.md), [first candidate hosted gate](https://github.com/larsbx/pisot-substitution-conjecture-research/actions/runs/42517853730).

- **2026-11-08 — Correction to the R1 core-instance priority experiment above.** Candidate `862266826ced4d717d4c04d207ff99273d0f4d74` selected the core finite instances but still exceeded the added no-choice axiom budget; all 47 theorem inspections and fourteen executable checks passed again. The ineffective priority is removed. Transitive axiom inventories include erased proof fields, so the corrected gate prints and checks the standard `propext` / `Classical.choice` / `Quot.sound` budget while separately requiring generic validator C emission and actual Boolean execution. It still rejects `sorryAx`, extra axioms, altered source-completeness contracts and vacuous replacements. No original condition, theorem statement or proof body is weakened. - **2026-11-06 — Concentration attack (four routes): not proved; degree four reduced to one configuration.** Proved, and checked by the author: Theorem A. At `r(C) = 4` the `(2,2,0)` sector is excluded for every PIP (Wielandt plus `α^{2ℓ} = γ^{2ℓ}` would make `α^{2ℓ}` rational); the cocycle is gauge or anti-gauge; and `Q₄D = L∘P`, so every state has `K₄ ≠ 0`. What remains is configuration (R): unimodular, `det 1`, gauge-trivial. Also proved: a strict component with `K₂ ≡ 0` yields two `σ^p`-fixed points with syndetically 2-binomially-equivalent prefixes, so `b₂ = p` implies Concentration; Tribonacci follows via Lejeune–Rigo–Rosenfeld Thm 29, which is computer-assisted (an imported dependency). Negative controls, pinned natively: `T13 = (1222200110120, 2201121220001)` puts a nonzero `K₃` in the det-sector of the PIP `M0 = [[5,21,1],[6,26,1],[6,24,1]]`, so no single-pair argument closes degree three; and `011/2/120` has `0112201 ∼₂ 1200112`, so `b₂ = p` is not a general route. Scratch only: the Heisenberg reformulation (lifted hub disjointness, for which no method is known), and windows with no `K₂ ≡ 0` strict system for states ≤ 13 (images ≤ 3) or ≤ 11 (images ≤ 4). Every route stops at the literal factorization, i.e. at strong coincidence. Do not retry: single-pair degree-3 lemmas; mid-area, renormalized displacement or subtile-measure arguments for `K₂ ≡ 0`. Locator: [Concentration attack](c4-concentration-attack-2026-11-06.md), `kernel/tests/test_c4_concentration_controls.mojo`.

- **2026-11-06 — Lean formalization of the Concentration attack.** Four `proof/PscVerif` modules, all passing the proof-bank axiom audit (442 declarations, 262 theorems, standard axioms only):
  - `Binomial`: `K₂` block additivity and the finite-form reduction lemma;
  - `ConcentrationAlgebra`: the eigen-functional Galois lemma, and the factorization and injectivity steps of Theorem A(iv);
  - `Degree4Sector`: the `(2,2,0)` exclusion, from `α^{2ℓ} = ᾱ^{2ℓ}` to rationality to contradiction, with no Galois-group machinery;
  - `ConcentrationControls`: both negative controls, by kernel `decide`.

  Not formalized: Wielandt's theorem (absent from Mathlib), so Theorem A(iii) and the cycle relation; the Perron-simplicity derivation of the `ker P` hypothesis; cyclicity of `Λ²M`; the limit to one-sided fixed points. Pitfall: `lake env lean` cannot see a new project module until `lake build PscVerif.<Module>` has built it. Locator: [Concentration attack §8](c4-concentration-attack-2026-11-06.md), `proof/PscVerif/PscVerif/{Binomial,ConcentrationAlgebra,Degree4Sector,ConcentrationControls}.lean`.

- **2026-11-06 — Lean: the remaining Concentration gaps closed, except the representation-theoretic inputs.** Seven more `proof/PscVerif` modules:
  - `PerronCore`: positivity and the `|x|`-eigenvector lemmas;
  - `Wielandt`: Wielandt's equality case, Theorem A(iii), the cycle relation, and the `(2,2,0)` exclusion end to end;
  - `PerronSimple`: the `ker P` hypothesis derived from Perron simplicity, using a real `β`-eigenvector in `ker P` and a ℚ-linear functional `ℝ → ℚ`, with no Perron–Frobenius existence theorem and no Galois theory;
  - `Cyclicity`: cyclic vectors; irreducibility of `χ_{Λ²M}` via `(Λ²M)ᵀPM = det M·P`;
  - `WeightModulus`: the modulus eliminations;
  - `FixedPointLimit`: limit words, with distinct first letters and syndetic `∼₂` cuts;
  - `M0Pisot`: the rational root theorem and IVT.

  The whole library now has 584 declarations and 394 theorems, standard axioms only. Two adversarial reviewers found one blocker (the modules were not yet in the root import closure, so `make_proof_bank.py --check` rejected them) and minor docstring overclaims and duplicated helpers; all are fixed. Not formalized: the `Lie₄` Witt weights, `Q_r S = Φ_r Q_r` and `ρ(S) ≤ β`, and the identification with an actual strict component. Locator: [Concentration attack §8](c4-concentration-attack-2026-11-06.md).

- **2026-11-06 — Lean: the Galois-orbit step of Theorem A(ii).** `proof/PscVerif/PscVerif/GaloisOrbit.lean`:
  - transitivity via `Normal.minpoly_eq_iff_mem_orbit` on the splitting field inside `ℂ`, so any exponent of a weight can be moved onto `β`, the conjugate again being a root of the rational `q`;
  - `(3,1,0)` forces a root of modulus `> β²`;
  - `(2,1,1)` forces `|d| = 1`;
  - `(2,2,0)` forces `|d| = 1`, `|α| = |γ|` and a non-real pair (if `α` were real, `γ = −α` and `α² ∈ ℚ`, contradicting the cubic).

  The modulus steps call `WeightModulus.weight_310` and `weight_220` at `‖β‖`, so there is no duplicated arithmetic. Non-vacuity is witnessed numerically by Tribonacci. Library: 603 declarations, 408 theorems, standard axioms only. With this, everything in Theorem A except the representation-theoretic inputs is formalized: the `Lie₄` weights, `Φ` semisimplicity, `Q_r S = Φ_r Q_r`, `ρ(S) ≤ β`, and the identification with an actual component. Locator: [Concentration attack §8](c4-concentration-attack-2026-11-06.md).

- **2026-11-06 — Lean: the word-level inputs of Theorem A.** Six more `proof/PscVerif` modules:
  - `Incidence`: the single home of the incidence matrix and the count, `n2` and `n3` substitution laws;
  - `CountBridge`: bridges to the older `Psc.N₂`/`N₃`/`K₂`/`K₃` and `ConcentrationControls.n3`;
  - `ParikhIntertwiner`: `P_C N_C = M P_C`, `λN_C = βλ`, `λ > 0`, and `ρ ≤ β` for every matrix dominated by `N_C`, also under subinvariance;
  - `Intertwiner2`: `K₂` push-forward, antisymmetry, `kw(σu,σv) = Λ²M·kw(u,v)`, block additivity;
  - `Intertwiner3`: `K₃` additivity for `∼₂` blocks and `K₃(σu,σv) = M^{⊗3}K₃(u,v)`;
  - `PrimitiveExpansion`: `count_iterate_subst = (inc s)^k`, and the reduction lemma, limit form, for a primitive `σ` via `ChildSystem.pow` (added to `Binomial`).

  Library: 741 declarations, 524 theorems, standard axioms only. A container restart killed the refactor workflow mid-run. The surviving partial edits were completed by hand, with one fix: `List.count` on pairs was elaborated with two different lawful `BEq` instances, so `Finset.sum_subset` would not unify until `convert` exposed the mismatch. Pitfall: σ itself need not lengthen strict states (`012/2/0` with state `(12, 21)`), which is why the power is needed; `PrimitiveExpansion.card_two_needed` is the one-letter negative control. - **2026-11-07 — Lean: the signed cocycle, vacuity controls, and a correction to the 2026-11-06 coverage claims.**
  - **Correction.** The 2026-11-06 note and `f6ca7b7` said `ρ(S) ≤ β` was formalized "for every matrix dominated by `N_C`, and `S` is one". That was false as typed: Lean's `Nc` counts raw ordered children (the oriented double cover), while the manuscript's `S = A − B` is indexed by normalised representatives.
  - **Now proved** (`proof/PscVerif/PscVerif/SignedCocycle.lean`): `A`, `B`, `N_C = A + B` and `S = A − B` on representatives. Every `S`-eigenvector lifts antisymmetrically to an `Nc`-eigenvector (`x̃(T) = x(T)`, `x̃(Tᵗ) = −x(T)`), so `ρ(S) ≤ β`. A second route goes through `λN_C = βλ` and `|S| ≤ N_C`. Thue–Morse (`C = {(01,10),(10,01)}`) gives non-vacuity, with `N_C = (2)` and `S = (0)`.
  - **Vacuity.** On two letters no irreducible noncoincident pair is 2-binomially equivalent (`PrimitiveExpansion.binary_no_equiv2`; elementary and standard, cf. Rigo–Salimov 2015), so the premise of the limit-form reduction lemma is empty there. On three letters that emptiness is the open "combinatorial Concentration". `thueMorse_control` meets every other hypothesis.
  - **Also new:** `ρ(Nc) = β` (`perron_root_childSystem`); `k3_children`; primitivity restated with `k ≥ 1` (`M⁰ = I` made every one-letter substitution "primitive").
  - **Hygiene:** `Binomial.Irreducible` renamed `IrreduciblePair` to avoid shadowing Mathlib's; the last duplicates removed. The 2026-11-06 Lean entry above names `ConcentrationControls.n3`; that definition is now deleted, and `k3` uses `Psc.Incidence.n3`.
  - **Tooling pitfall:** a plain `simp [chi, det3, m0]; ring` in `chi_is_charpoly` printed `PANIC at Lean.Expr.appArg!` on every build without failing it; it is replaced by `simp only … ; norm_num; ring`.

  Library: 836 declarations, 599 theorems, standard axioms only. Locator: [Concentration attack §8](c4-concentration-attack-2026-11-06.md).

- **2026-11-07 — Lean: zero-return reduction, `thm:parikh` rank clauses on representatives, signed intertwiners, `K₃ ∈ Lie₃`.**
  - **New modules** (`proof/PscVerif/PscVerif/`). `Reduction` proves `red` (cut at zero returns): its blocks, their uniqueness, the swap rule, and `redNormalisation`, which discharges `children_swap` for zero-return children. `ParikhRank` proves rank `d`, `m ≥ d`, `χ_M ∣ χ_{N_C}` over `ℚ` and `ℤ`, and similarity when `m = d`, all for an abstract intertwiner. `SignedIntertwiner` proves `Q₂S = Φ₂Q₂` and `Q₃S = Φ₃Q₃` on representatives. `Lie3` proves the shuffle relation and Ree at degree three on three letters (eight Lyndon brackets, dimension 8), and that `M^{⊗3}` preserves `Lie₃`.
  - **Vacuous variant retained, relabelled.** The first draft of `ParikhRank` stated every clause for the raw double cover `Nc`. Under any normalisation `|C| = 2|R|` (`card_state_eq_two_mul`), so its `m = d` clause can never fire (`no_equiv_fin3`). The clauses are now also proved on representatives (`prep_nrep`: `P_R N_C = M P_R`, under balance), and the double-cover ones are labelled as such.
  - **Finite observation.** A `Normalisation` forces `C` to be coincidence-free (`Normalisation.noncoincident`). So a `C` that still contains a coincidence block such as `(2,2)` admits none.
  - **Controls.** The control `exB` (`0↔1`, `C = {(0110,1001), swap}`) is algebra-only: its state is reducible (`exB_reducible`), and its declared children are not those of `red` (`exB_not_red`). `wT` (the identity on `(0112201, 1200112)`, built by `redSystem`) is the zero-return control with `K₃ ≠ 0`.
  - **Redundancy removed.** `k3_shuffle_right` is `k3_shuffle_left` at `(c,a,b)` (`ab ⧢ c = c ⧢ ab`). It is now derived, and so is `n2_mul_count`.
  - **Tooling pitfall.** Inside `first | ring | …`, a failing `ring` does not fail: it falls back to `ring_nf`, leaves the goal, and `first` commits to it. Use `ring1`.

  Library: 34 modules, 1049 declarations, 767 theorems, standard axioms only. Locator: [Concentration attack §8](c4-concentration-attack-2026-11-06.md).

- **2026-11-07 — Lean: descendants, kernel cofactor, degree-2 sector, cocycle spectrum, Perron–Frobenius, degree-4 defect, Lie weights.**
  - **New modules.** Eight modules join `PscVerif`: `Descendants`, `KernelCofactor`, `Degree2Sector`, `CocycleSpectrum`, `PerronExistence`, `Defect4`, `LieWeights` and `Defect4Lie`. Each was implemented, adversarially reviewed and fixed before integration. Coverage is in the §8 rows.
  - **Manuscript reading (`lem:descendants`).** The lemma holds in the child graph where the children of coincidence blocks are also formed. Read on `B_σ`, where coincidence blocks are terminal, it fails. For Fibonacci `0↦01, 1↦0`, `red(σ²01, σ²10)` contains `(1,1)`, which is not a vertex of `B_σ`. The two graphs agree on noncoincident states and are finite together. The manuscript is archived and not edited here. Locator: `Psc.Descendants.fib_descR_two`, `fib_bpaStatesNC`, `mem_bpaStatesNC_iff`, `finite_bpaStates_iff`.
  - **Manuscript reading (`lem:conc-subst` (iii) at `r = 4`).** As printed, it is false on repeated letters at degree four, as at degree three (D2 of `docs/verification-architecture.md`). Products of scattered counts are infiltration products. Their corrections have degree `< 4` and cancel under `∼₃`, so the relations of `K₄` hold. Locator: `Psc.Defect4.count_mul_n3`, `n3_mul_count`, `n2_mul_n2`.
  - **Finite observations.**
    - At degree four `ab ⧢ cd` is not redundant (`LieWeights.r1_not_shuffleAnnih4`), unlike degree three, where `ab ⧢ c` is a re-indexing.
    - A binary `∼₂` pair satisfies every `ab ⧢ cd` relation (`Defect4.k4_shuffle_two_binary`), so its negative control must be ternary.
    - `ρ(N_C) = β` on representatives needs only `ℓ > 0` and balance, not irreducibility of `N_C` (`PerronExistence.perron_root_rep_of_primitive`, `KernelCofactor.perron_root_of_intertwine`).
    - Given a normalisation, balance makes the nonempty-track hypothesis `hne` redundant (`Degree2Sector.ne_nil_of_balanced`).
  - **Counter-calibrations.**
    - Strong connectivity is needed in the strict bound and in the gauge criterion (`CocycleSpectrum.neg_sc_strict`, `neg_sc_gauge`).
    - Primitivity is needed in the equality case: `pNorm` has eigenvalue `i`, of modulus `β = 1` (`neg_primitive`).
    - Each of surjectivity, strong connectivity and irreducibility is needed for the cofactor and simplicity statements (`KernelCofactor.neg_surjective_needed`, `neg_sc_needed`, `wT_no_cofactor`).
  - **Vacuity, stated.** The representative theorems of `KernelCofactor` and `Degree2Sector` have no control meeting every hypothesis. They are reductio lemmas about a hypothetical strict component. Their controls reach every hypothesis except irreducibility of `χ_M` (`wT_rep_hyps`, `exA_sector`); `plastic` meets the matrix-level ones.
  - **Caught in review.**
    - `LieWeights` first compared Lyndon counts with a Witt quotient taken by integer floor division, so `⌊3/4⌋ = 0` passed. It now proves the exact identity `r·m(κ) = wittSum κ`.
    - A draft minimality claim about the `∼₃` control pair `w4Pair` came from an unpinned scratch search and was withdrawn. So was a scratch BPA search figure; neither is cited.
  - **Tooling pitfalls.**
    - A module outside `PscVerif.lean` has no olean, so sibling modules cannot import it. The `K₄ ∈ Lie₄` bridge (`Defect4Lie`) had to wait for integration.
    - Passing a concrete representation to a lemma generic in `[AddCommGroup V]` can time out at `whnf` unless `(V := …)` is given explicitly.

  Library: 42 modules, 2038 declarations, 1575 theorems, standard axioms only. Locator: [Concentration attack §8](c4-concentration-attack-2026-11-06.md).

The rational statements are the instances; `k3Vec` stays over `ℚ`. `LieWeights` lost its degree-three restatements (`Vec3R`, `ShuffleAnnih3`, `shuffleAnnih3Space`, `phi3R`, `lyndon3Coeff`, `lyndon3`, the `dual3` transport through `ℚ`-linear functionals, their `rfl` bridges) and uses the `Lie3` names; `phi3R_*` lemmas are now `phi3_*`.

  - **Tooling pitfall.** A finite certificate checked by `decide +kernel` over `ℚ` generalizes to every commutative ring by checking it over `ℤ` and transporting along `Int.castRingHom` (`Lie3.shuffleAnnih_br_int`, as `LieWeights.shuffleAnnih4_br_int` already did); the earlier transport from `ℚ` to a field through separating `ℚ`-linear functionals was only needed because `Lie3` was not generic.
  - **Not shared, on purpose: the degree-3 / degree-4 content gradings.** `LieWeights.homog3`/`homog4`, `proj3`/`proj4` and their pointwise lemmas (`proj*_apply`, `proj*_mem_homog*`, `proj*_of_mem`, `proj*_of_mem_ne`, `sum_proj*`, `iSupIndep_homog*`) stay as two near-verbatim copies. The abstract layer (`iSupIndep_of_proj`, `iSup_inf_eq_of_proj`, `span_inf_eq_of_proj`) is already shared; sharing the rest would need one index type for both degrees, but `Lie3.Vec3 R` and `Vec4 R` are curried function types whose `proj*_apply` lemmas are `rfl`, so a common lemma means re-basing both on `(Fin r → Fin 3) → R` through `uncurry` equivalences and re-proving every downstream `simp`/`rfl` use. Judged not cheap; do not redo the analysis without a third degree to share with.

The certificate only divides by `3`; the intermediate hypothesis `(3 : F) ≠ 0` is not stated (the `linear_combination` steps with `1/3` close by `ring1` only under `CharZero`).

- **2026-11-07 — Lean: `Psc.SectorBridge`, the shared steps of `thm:deg23` (iii) and `thm:sieve`.**
  - **Diagonalization needs only three distinct roots.** `exists_conj_diagonal` diagonalizes a rational `3 × 3` `M` over any characteristic-zero field from any three pairwise distinct roots of `χ_M` (eigenvector matrix; `Module.End.eigenvectors_linearIndependent'`, `Matrix.linearIndependent_cols_iff_isUnit`); irreducibility enters only to produce the roots (`exists_distinct_roots`). So the bridge theorems `root_q3`, `root_q4` take the roots, in any order, as data: `(β, α, γ)` of `Psc.GaloisOrbit` can be plugged in directly as `x = ![β, α, γ]`.
  - **Restriction to an invariant subspace divides `χ_φ`** (`charpoly_restrict_dvd`): not in Mathlib at this pin; it is `KernelCofactor.charpoly_eq_mul_restrict` for `W.mkQ`, plus a `subst` lemma moving `φ.restrict` from `ker W.mkQ` to `W` (`Submodule.ker_mkQ` is propositional, not definitional).
  - **Tooling pitfall.** `(φ.restrict _).charpoly` with `φ` on a concrete submodule type (`↥(span ℚ (range lyndon))`) and the restriction to `LinearMap.range Qm` fails to elaborate (instance-path mismatch `Field.toSemifield` vs `CommRing.toCommSemiring`, then a `whnf` timeout), while the same expression elaborates in a section generic in `V`. Fix used: define the polynomial once generically (`imCharpoly h`) and instantiate it (`q3`, `q4`).
  - **Not done here, ready to build on.** With `x = ![β, α, γ]` and a bound `‖z‖ ≤ ‖β‖` on the roots of `χ_S`: at `r = 4`, `exists_weight_root_q4` plus `GaloisOrbit.orbit_310_excluded` / `orbit_211_forces_unit` / `orbit_220_forces_complex_unit` should give `|det M| = 1`; at `r = 3`, `orbit_exponent_on_beta` excludes every `(2,1,0)` root of `q₃` (a root `β²δ`, `‖βδ‖ > 1`), so `q₃ = (X − det M)^{rank Q₃}`, and the multiplicity two of `det M` in `χ(Φ₃)` (`LieWeights.charpoly_phi3Lie_explicit`, distinct nonzero roots) should give `rank Q₃ ≤ 2` without semisimplicity. Only `im Q₃ ⊆ ker(Φ₃ − det M)` needs semisimplicity of `Φ₃|_{im Q₃}`.
  - **No full control** for `root_q3`, `root_q4`, `exists_weight_root_q*`: the state-level controls `wT`, `w4` use the identity substitution (`χ_M = (X − 1)³`); `M0`, `plastic` meet irreducibility but carry no child system. On `wT`, `w4` (`S = (1)`) the divisibility is an equality, `q_r = χ_S` (`wT_q3_eq`, `w4_q4_eq`).

  Library: 43 modules, 2099 declarations, 1632 theorems, standard axioms only. Locator: [Concentration attack §8](c4-concentration-attack-2026-11-06.md), `proof/PscVerif/PscVerif/SectorBridge.lean`.

- **2026-11-07 — Correction to the `Psc.SectorBridge` entry above (review).**
  - **The "No full control" bullet is wrong for `root_q3`, `root_q4`, `galoisOrbit_input_q4`.** They need only three pairwise distinct roots of `χ_M`, not irreducibility. The 3-cycle relabelling `0 ↦ 1 ↦ 2 ↦ 0` (`cycSubst`, `χ_M = X³ − 1`, roots `1, ζ, ζ²`) on the three relabellings of `wTPair` (resp. `w4Pair`) and their swaps meets every hypothesis: `cyc3_root_q3`, `cyc4_root_q4`, `cyc4_galoisOrbit_input`. It is a permutation substitution, so this shows joint satisfiability only. Only `exists_weight_root_q3`, `exists_weight_root_q4` (`χ_M` irreducible, with a child system) lack a full control; they are reductio lemmas.
  - **"A listed-shape weight is not numerically a pure power" is false without the Pisot moduli, not merely unproved.** For the irreducible `X³ − 2`, any three distinct roots give `x₀x₁x₂ = x₀³` and `x₀²x₁x₂ = x₀⁴`. `mono_eq_pow_of_cube_eq` proves this for any three distinct cube roots of one value, by `linear_combination`; `cycX_mono_pure` applies it to the cycle control. `Shape4.le_three` and `Shape3.le_two` constrain `κ`, not the value.
  - **Downstream match narrowed.** `Psc.WeightModulus` takes modulus inequalities, not a polynomial `q`. `galoisOrbit_input_q4` supplies only `habc`, `hq` of the `GaloisOrbit` shape theorems; the rest (`Irreducible χ_M`, the Pisot moduli, `1 ≤ ‖βαγ‖`, `hbound`) is listed in its docstring.
  - **Tooling pitfall.** `tools/make_proof_bank.py` records a SHA-256 for each `.lean` file, so any edit after generation makes `--check` fail ("Stale proof bank"). Regenerate after the last edit.
  - DRY: `m0_irreducible` was a duplicate of `Psc.KernelCofactor.ctrl_irreducible` and is now removed. The twin proofs are factored into `linearCombination_intertwine`, `range_linearCombination_ne_bot` and `imCharpoly_eq_of_card_eq_one`.

  Library: 43 modules, 2143 declarations, 1664 theorems, standard axioms only. Locator: `proof/PscVerif/PscVerif/SectorBridge.lean` (Controls section).

- **2026-11-07 — Lean: `thm:deg23` (iii) and `thm:sieve` at `r = 4` end to end (`Psc.Degree3Sector`, `Psc.Degree4Floor`).**
  - **Closed.** The route left open in the `SectorBridge` entry above is now formalized. `deg23_iii` proves all four clauses over `ℚ`: every root of `q₃` is `det M`, `q₃ = (X − det M)^{rank Q₃}`, `im Q₃ ⊆ ker(Φ₃ − det M)` and `rank Q₃ ≤ 2`. `sieve4` proves `|det M| = 1` and that `χ_S` has a root of modulus `‖β‖`. `theoremA_of_primitive` gives Theorem A(i)–(iii) at `r = 4` for a primitive Pisot `σ` with strongly connected `N_C`: `S = d·DN_CD`, with `dβ` an eigenvalue. These are reductio lemmas: their hypotheses describe a hypothetical strict component.
  - **Route differs from the manuscript.** The proof of `thm:deg23` (iii) goes through Galois-stable rational constituents. Lean instead uses the Galois-orbit lemma (`GaloisOrbit.orbit_exponent_on_beta`): a `(2,1,0)` root `β²δ` of `q₃` has modulus `> β`, since `‖βδ‖ = |det M| / ‖other root‖ > 1`. Semisimplicity is used only in kernel form, `ker(Φ₃ − μ)^k = ker(Φ₃ − μ)`.
  - **Finite observations.**
    - The Perron root of a primitive `M` with a Pisot root `β` is `β` (`Degree4Floor.perron_eq_pisot`).
    - Under the `deg23` hypotheses, any positive left eigenvalue of `M` is `β` (`Degree3Sector.perron_eq_root`).
    - There is no `r = 4` child system over the `det = −2` substitution `M0` (`Degree4Floor.m0_no_degree4`).
    - The census state `(011212001, 120011120)` of `011/2/120` lies in no `∼₂` normalised child system over that substitution (`Degree3Sector.census_not_mem`). The pairing of that state with that substitution rests on a scratch inflation check, which is not cited as evidence.
  - **DRY at integration.** The two modules had been written in parallel. The helpers they duplicated now live once in `Psc.SectorBridge`:
    - `vec3_injective`, `vec3_roots`, `root_cases`;
    - `det_eq_mul_roots`, `one_le_norm_mul_roots`;
    - `M0_det`;
    - `exists_eigen_of_root_ssign`, `ssign_root_bound_of_perron`.

    `Degree3Sector.roots_ssign_le` is now a one-line instance of `ssign_root_bound_of_perron`.

  Library: 45 modules, 2368 declarations, 1875 theorems, standard axioms only. Locator: [Concentration attack §8](c4-concentration-attack-2026-11-06.md).

- **2026-11-07 — Stop/go gate for solidifying the scratch census (A) and the outside-PIP strict-system search (B).**
  - **Prior art not previously in the repository: the minimal-length question is the `k`-deck problem.** Equal-length words have the same `k`-deck iff they are `k`-binomially equivalent. The least length `S(k)` with two distinct such words is `2, 4, 7, 12, 16, 30` for `k = 1..6`: Chrisnata–Kiah–Rao–Vardy–Yaakobi–Yao, ISCIT 2019, Table I and Theorem 7. Manvel et al. 1991 give `S(k) ≤ 2^k` (Thue–Morse) and a reduction to binary words, so `S(k)` does not depend on the alphabet; this is as reported by Golm–Nahvi–Gabrys–Milenkovic 2022, and the 1991 paper was not read. Do not claim novelty for unrestricted minimal lengths; only the irreducibility specialisation is unstudied in the sources read.
  - **The binary half of A is decided.** For `k ≥ 2` it is empty (`binary_no_equiv2`). For `k = 1` there are exactly `2·C_{n−1}` ordered irreducible pairs, by bicoloured Motzkin paths, all with `K₂ ≠ 0`. Keep the binary run only as an enumerator calibration, against that closed form and against Table I `D_3(n)`, `D_4(n)` for `n ≤ 20`.
  - **The literature fixes the degree window.** In the ternary window `n ≤ 15`, `DEGREE_FIVE_PLUS` can only be degree exactly 5. This is an import, reported only, never used to relabel.
  - **The commutation theorem always yields reducible pairs.** Whiteland 2021 (RSW Theorem 2.3): `xy ∼_k yx` iff `x ∼_{k−1} y`. Its outputs, including Ochsenschläger's Thue–Morse blocks, are negative controls of the irreducibility filter.
  - **Decisions.**
    - A, ternary, `k = 2, 3`, `n ≤ 12`: proceed narrowed, in Mojo, by signature bucketing.
    - B, both-outside irreducible non-Pisot: stop (R3).
    - B, one-outside: stop seeded closures (Corollary B(iii), Proposition B′).
    - B, reducible: stop a new `K₂ ≠ 0` search; the 28 pinned carriers suffice.
    - `K₂ ≡ 0` greatest-fixed-point window over the one-outside and reducible non-synchronizing specimens: proceed, fused with A, labelled exploratory. A complete pair list replaces the scratch `MINL = 7` shortcut.
  - **Finite observations, scratch.**
    - The 59,319 substitutions with images ≤ 3 split as: 33,318 primitive; 4,554 PIP; 3,960 both-outside; 3,216 one-outside, 1,356 of them with `|det| = 1`; 21,588 reducible.
    - The non-synchronizing counts 1,542 / 1,812 / 1,704 / 10,134 sum to the 15,192 of the 2026-11-06 window search.
    - The endpoint filter of that search is justified by `heads_ne` and its mirror. It is the eventually-constant case of cut-pair Theorem A(1),(3), which is prior; Theorem A(4), strong-coincidence witnesses, is a further seed-independent filter.
    - For Parikh-collinear `σ`, `M^{⊗r}` kills `Lie_r` (`r ≥ 2`), so every defect-intertwiner constraint is void there.
    - The six length-7 seeds form one class under relabelling, reversal and swap.
  - **Candidate control, not checked in Lean.** The pinned carrier `012/120/201` (`χ = x²(x − 3)`, 3-state strict system) appears to meet every hypothesis of `Degree4Floor.ssign_root_bound_of_primitive`, which has only the degenerate control `plastic_empty_control`.
  - **Tooling pitfall.** pdftotext garbles superscripts in the `k`-deck papers ("`2k ≤ S(k) ≤ 2k`" for `2^k`). Read bounds from the tables or from the original.

  Locator: [solidify stop/go](c4-solidify-stop-go-2026-11-07.md); scratch probes under the session scratchpad `wf9/probe/`.

- **2026-11-07 — Experiment A: the binomial-pair census (integrated 2026-11-09).**
  - **Scratch figures replaced.** The scratch ternary counts 12 / 24 / 6 of irreducible `∼₃` pairs at `n = 12, 13, 14` are reproduced exactly by `kernel/binomial_pair_census.mojo` (pinned for `n ≤ 13` by `kernel/tests/test_binomial_pair_census.mojo`; the `n = 14` row is recorded, not pinned). The withdrawn minimality remark on `Psc.Defect4.w4Pair` is restored as a citation of this census (no `∼₃` irreducible ternary pair of length `≤ 11`; exactly 12 at length 12, one `G`-orbit with least element `w4Pair`), not as a Lean statement.
  - **Finite observations, do not recompute.** Ternary minimal lengths: `K₂ = 0` at 7, `∼₃` at 12, no `∼₄` for `n ≤ 14`. The six length-7 `K₂ = 0` pairs are the six seeds of `kernel/psc/seeds.mojo`, one orbit. Binary: `2·C_{n−1}` ordered irreducible pairs and no `K₂ = 0` pair for `n ≤ 20` (`binary_no_equiv2`), Table I `D_3`, `D_4` of Chrisnata et al. reproduced.
  - **Remaining gaps.** The degree-3 / degree-4 split at `n = 13, 14` has no independent replay (the oracle replays only their `K₂ = 0` totals); minimal lengths without irreducibility are the `k`-deck numbers (no novelty claimed).

  Locator: [binomial-pair census](c4-binomial-minimal-pairs-2026-11-07.md); `kernel/tests/test_binomial_pair_census.mojo`.

- **2026-11-07 — C4 off the PIP corpus: the `K₂ = 0` window search (experiment B of the solidify stop/go, fused with A; integrated 2026-11-09).**
  - **Withdrawn, not adapted.** An interrupted draft of this step did three things that contradicted gate §4.6/§4.7 and AGENTS.md "do not re-enumerate": it labelled swap-seeded explorations in the irreducible non-Pisot class as verdicts, it built an all-pairs length-7 core, and it enumerated the `K₂ = 0` pairs a second time. All three were deleted, and none of their figures is cited. In that class a seeded run can only cap (R3, Corollary B(iii)).
  - **Window observation, not a verdict.** Among the 11,838 non-synchronizing one-outside (1,704) and reducible (10,134) specimens with images ≤ 3, no strict system has `K₂ = 0` on every state and every state of length ≤ 13. The same holds for length ≤ 14, but that rests on A's unpinned `n = 14` row. Every first-pass survivor dies in one removal round at `L = 11, 13, 14`.
  - **Scope of the reproduction.** Only the non-PIP part (13,650 of 15,192 specimens) of the 2026-11-06 scratch `k2zero_strict.c` window is reproduced. The `K₂ = 0` window of the 1,542 non-synchronizing PIP specimens remains scratch, and so does the length ≤ 11 window with images ≤ 4.
  - **Verdicts.** By Theorem A(4) and its reversal, 5,754 domain specimens (456 one-outside, 5,298 reducible) have no strict system at all. Of these, 870 (216 + 654) are decided by right witnesses only, so they also rest on Lemma 4 of the note. All 15,114 synchronizing off-corpus specimens are decided the same way.
  - **Do not recompute.**
    - At `L = 11` all 156 first-pass survivors of the domain and R3 passes are length-7 pairs. Each has one child, of length 11, and that child fails in turn.
    - The 28 pinned reducible carriers have `K₂ ≠ 0` on all 123 ordered closure states, so the `K₂ = 0` window cannot see them.
    - The exact partition, previously scratch, is now pinned: PIP 4,554 / 1,542; one-outside 3,216 / 1,704; both-outside 3,960 / 1,812; reducible 21,588 / 10,134.
  - **Independent replay of A's `n = 12..14` `K₂ = 0` totals.** `oracles/python/c4_closed_component_search.py universe 14` gives 50,406 / 323,274 / 2,168,652 in 1 min 40 s. The split between degree 3 and degree 4 at `n = 13, 14` is still Mojo only.
  - **Tooling pitfall.** `psc.pisot_screen.roots_outside_unit_circle` refuses three cubics at a Routh singularity: `x³ − 6x − 3` (one-outside, 18 specimens) and the PIP cubics `x³ − x² − 2x − 2` and `x³ − 2x² − x − 2` (810 specimens). There the Sturm–Tarski count (`conjugates_outside`) is the exact method, and a refusal is not a verdict.
  - **Fail-closed fix.** The R3′ consistency check refused a reducible cubic only on its `r(C) = 3k` branch; it now refuses on every branch.
  - **No strict system found, so no new Lean control.** The search found no carrier. Its find path was exercised only on known closed sets: the Lean `cyc3Norm` and `ex:nonpisot` (`1/012/1`, `χ_M = x(x − 2)(x + 1)`, reducible), the manuscript's known non-Pisot strict component. No Lean control was added from the search.
  - **Gaps unchanged.** There is no control that drops only Pisot, and no primitive literal `K₂ ≡ 0` control on three letters (gate §5).

  Locator: [closed-component search](c4-closed-component-search-2026-11-07.md); `kernel/tests/test_c4_closed_component_search.mojo`.

- **2026-11-07 — Lean: strict systems, the `comp:defect` catalogue, the leave-one-out controls, and the small solid items (integration of `Psc.StrictSystem`, `Psc.Degree3Catalogue`, `Psc.ReductioControls`; integrated 2026-11-09).**
  - **Scratch or prose figures, now theorems.**
    - "The `R1`-only annihilator has dimension `81 − 57 = 24`, a computation of the generator" (`Psc.LieWeights` docstring): now `finrank_r1Annih4Space`, with the explicit basis `r1Basis` (the 18 Lyndon brackets and the six concatenation products `[x,y]·[z,w]` of degree-two brackets) and an integral coefficient map; the 57 remaining coordinates are `linear_combination` certificates generated in exact rationals by an untrusted scratch script (`wf9/integ/r1gen.py`) and checked in Lean.
    - "`X³ − 2` is irreducible (Eisenstein at `2`), not formalized here" (`Psc.SectorBridge`): now `X_pow_three_sub_two_irreducible_int`, `X_pow_three_sub_two_irreducible`, with the negative control `cube_root_two_control` (`cubeSubst = 1/2/00`).
    - "Under the Pisot moduli the `(1,1,1)` weight is not a pure power; not proved" (`Psc.SectorBridge`): now `Psc.GaloisOrbit.mono111_ne_cube`, extended to the `(2,1,1)` weights by `mono211_ne_pow4` and the matrix form `Psc.SectorBridge.pure_power_excluded`.
    - "`dim_ℚ ker(Φ₃ − det M) = 2`: only `≤ 2`" (`Psc.Degree3Sector`): now `finrank_ker_sub_det_eq_two`. No base change of rank is needed: the root multiplicity of `det M` in the rational `χ(Φ₃)` is two, `LinearMap.finrank_maxGenEigenspace_eq` turns it into a dimension, and the kernel form of semisimplicity identifies the generalized eigenspace with the kernel.
    - The census pairing "rests on a scratch inflation check" (2026-11-07 Lean entry above; `Psc.Degree3Sector` docstrings): now `Psc.Degree3Catalogue.catalogue_spec` for all 24 occurrences, with `class9a_sigmaA_control` for the length-21 inflation.
    - "A scratch search over images of length `≤ 3` found no such component (capped runs)" (`Psc.KernelCofactor`): the sentence is withdrawn, and the scratch figure stays unverified; the docstring now cites `C4StandingCorpus` and the closed-component search note instead. The same for "the stop/go note's window search is scratch" (`Psc.ReductioControls`).
    - The informal argument of the `Psc.Degree2Sector` "Not proved here" bullet now names its formal pieces (`StrictSystem.deg2_sector`, `StrictSystem.mismatch_density`, Theorem A(4) `not_witness`, `plastic_vacuous`, `C4StandingCorpus`); what stays informal is that the hypotheses force Pisot, the passage from mismatch density to a nonproductive overlap, and formal productivity from PDS.
  - **Finite observations and manuscript-reading points, do not recompute.**
    - The moduli alone do not exclude two of the nine `(2,1,1)`-versus-fourth-power equalities: `(β, α, γ) = (729/250, 1/2, 9/10)` has `1 < β`, `α, γ < 1`, `βαγ ≥ 1` and `βα²γ = γ⁴` (`Psc.GaloisOrbit.moduli_211_control`). They fall to the Galois step (`mono211_ne_pow4_galois`, via the rational relation `β⁴α⁵ = (βαγ)³`). The prose remark of `Psc.SectorBridge` covered the `(1,1,1)` case only.
    - With the symmetrized products `u·v + v·u` of degree-two brackets as the six extra basis vectors, every choice of six coordinate words beside the 18 Lyndon words gives a coordinate block of determinant `0` or `±8` (checked over all choices inside the support), so no integral coordinate-reading coefficient map exists for that basis; the plain products `v·u` give a unimodular one, whose first 18 forms are exactly `lyndon4Coeff`.
    - `Psc.ReductioControls.rotSubst` is `Psc.StrictSystem.cycSubst` (`012/120/201`), and `rotSys 0` is the swap closure of the strict component `cycC`, with `R = cycC` (`rotSubst_eq_cycSubst`, `rot0_states_eq_cycC`, `rot0_reps_eq_cycC`, `rot0_strictComponent`): the `Irr`-dropped rows of the `KernelCofactor` and `Degree2Sector` tables are met by an actual strict component over a primitive substitution with reducible `χ_M`.
    - Each of the 12 catalogue automata has exactly 226 ordered vertices, 224 noncoincident, 4 of them `∼₂`, and every catalogue state returns to itself in 7 steps (`Psc.Degree3Catalogue.catalogue_card`, `OccSpec.recurrent`).
    - `m0Subst` carries no strict system (an instance of `thm:global-sync`: level-1 witnesses for all pairs), so `Degree4Floor.m0_no_degree4` has bite only at the `ChildSystem` level; the exclusion forms of `Psc.StrictSystem` have no demonstrated bite beyond Theorem A(4) (`no_strictSystem_deg2_odd_control`, `_deg3_control`, `_deg4_control`).
    - The sink step needs nonerasure: under `0, 1 ↦ ε`, `{(01, 10)}` is a strict system with no strict component (`erase_control`).
  - **DRY.** `orbit_211_root` and `aeval_conj_eq_zero` moved from `Psc.Degree4Floor` to `Psc.GaloisOrbit`, where `orbit_211_forces_unit` and `im_ne_zero_of_norm_eq` now use them; the old names remain as deprecated aliases. Not done, recorded: moving the `Psc.Degree3Catalogue` helpers (`phi3_ne_smul_of_coord_nat`, `k3Vec_ne_of_coord`, `cubic_certificate`, `not_mem_of_coord`) into `Psc.Degree3Sector`, and moving `isAntiGauge_walk` into `Psc.CocycleSpectrum`.
  - **Tooling pitfalls.**
    - `Psc.SectorBridge.cycSubst` (the 3-cycle relabelling `1/2/0`) and `Psc.StrictSystem.cycSubst` (`012/120/201`) share a short name and differ.
    - Proving `r1Basis_mem` with `fin_cases i; first | exact … (lyndon4_mem _)` times out at `whnf`: unifying `lyndon4 ?i` with a bracket is expensive. List the 24 members explicitly.
    - In a case split over `GaloisOrbit.IsPerm3`, `subst` on `h : x = α` eliminates `α`, not `x`; rewrite with `rw [h] at …` instead.
    - A Python search over all `C(63, 6)` sets of six coordinate words with exact `Fraction` inversion does not finish; use the block structure (the Lyndon brackets are unitriangular at the Lyndon words) and search the `6 × 6` complement.
    - `lake env lean` on a module compiles against the built `.olean` files of its imports, which are stale after an upstream edit: build upstream first.
    - Evaluating all 12 catalogue closures in one `decide +kernel` ran out of memory at about 13.5 GB; evaluate the two class representatives and transfer by relabelling (`bpaStatesNC_conj`). `simp` with `Intertwiner3.k3_swap` loops; use `rw`.
    - In this session's shallow clone, 30 pytest cases fail identically on a clean `HEAD` worktree, so they are not caused by this integration: the 27 `tests/test_class_d_*` cases (`git show` of a commit outside the shallow history), `tests/test_recorded_data_integrity.py::test_every_evidence_packet_is_pinned`, and both `test_proof_bank.py::test_private_proofs_reconcile_…` cases (the library already had three `private theorem`s at `HEAD`, which the test does not expect). Running `pytest` while `kernel/run_tests.sh` rewrites `kernel/build/claim-receipts.tsv` makes `test_claim_governance.py::test_status_surfaces_agree_with_the_ledger` read a partial receipt file and fail; run them in sequence.

- **2026-11-09 — Branch hygiene: PR #504 had merged on 2026-11-06, and 11 later commits were pushed to its branch without CI.** The Concentration attack, the Lean formalization rounds and the solidify round all landed after the merge, so no workflow ran on them. A force-push to restart the branch was refused earlier in the session, so `origin/main` (178 commits, through #520) is merged into the branch instead. A PR from it diffs only the new work. The conflicts were the root import list of `PscVerif.lean` (union: `OverlapContraction`, `OverlapPacket` and `PhysicalMass` from main), this ledger (both sides kept) and the generated proof bank (regenerated). The merged library has 51 modules, 3,527 declarations and 2,661 theorems, standard axioms only; the proof-bank, ledger, governance, markdown and data-integrity gates pass. **Pitfall:** before pushing follow-up work, check whether the branch's PR is still open. A merged PR runs no CI on later pushes.

The session's modules had added three: `GaloisOrbit.of_eq`, `WeightModulus.cube_bound` and `Degree3Sector.aeval_of_eq`, the last a private duplicate of the first. Fix: made `of_eq` and `cube_bound` public and deleted the duplicate, whose six uses now call `Psc.GaloisOrbit.of_eq`. The test is unchanged. **Pitfall:** do not use `private` in `PscVerif`, and run `tests/test_proof_bank.py` after adding Lean modules.

Weighted hub area instead forces a genuine physical overlap in a supplied flat strict component, with zero hub defect and possibly unequal source indices. - **2026-11-12 — the compact-neighborhood step separates all-cut uniformity from pointwise convergence.** Finite filter intersections combine the actual distinct-label pairs and a fixed finite strict carrier before selecting one generation threshold; the carrier-specific adapter needs only intersections indexed by its actual CutPair labels, and compactness selects a finite part of any open cover. The Lean controls prove the closed axis/hyperbola obstruction, the compact singleton non-open-target obstruction, and pointwise-but-not-uniform moving spikes. The result concerns original common cut vectors and supplies no count or positive measure. Locator: [uniform cut capture and formal controls](c4-compact-neighborhood-2026-11-12.md).

The third unchanged verifier completed its entire build and axiom audit at `6915e8d592b7ea5cb7c6950249811e354dc6c3e7`. Preserve raw log whitespace with deterministic compressed containers, and pin nested evidence paths with `./` in the required `SHA256SUMS`; the two earlier cut-prefix packets needed checksum metadata only, with all prior bytes preserved. - **2026-11-09 — Proposition B′ and Corollary B (i) are composed in Lean (`PscVerif.BPrime`); `BPrimeCore`, `BPrimeBinding` and `BPrime` are in the root import closure.** In the audit's corrected scope (one-sided fixed point `IsFixed s u`, nonempty prefix `W`, `σ` primitive with nonerasing derived, `χ_M` irreducible over `ℚ`, `I(W)` finite), `bprime` gives `‖z‖ ≤ 1` for every root `z ≠ β`. On two or more letters, `bprime_isPisotOrSalem` shows that `β > 1` is an algebraic integer with minimal polynomial `χ_M`. On three letters, `bprime_cubic_isPisot` gives `IsPisot β`. The contrapositives are `corB_infinite` (some root `z ≠ β` with `‖z‖ > 1`) and `corB_infinite_cubic` (irreducible, non-Pisot cubic). For the audit specimen `0 ↦ 01, 1 ↦ 2, 2 ↦ 00`, `zetaU_IW_infinite` shows that the `bpa-0` of `σ^ω(0)` does not terminate, with every hypothesis discharged in Lean. Conversely, `one_letter_control` (`0 ↦ 00`, `u = 0^∞`, `W = 0`) meets every hypothesis of `bprime` at once, finiteness of `I(0) ⊆ {(0, 0)}` included; its conclusion is empty there. The receipts are local only: `lake build` (no warnings) and `ProofBankAudit.lean` (54 modules, 3,911 declarations, 2,999 theorems, standard axioms). The proof bank has 1,803 named proofs and `tests/test_proof_bank.py` passes. There is no hosted CI receipt yet, and no claim status changed (B′ is not a governed claim). Open: Corollary B (ii)–(iii), periodic points, non-prefix seeds, `I(W)` finite for any irreducible specimen, the strict disc in degree `≥ 4`, refinement of `kernel/psc/bpa.mojo`, and novelty. Side findings:
  - The specimen's least primitivity exponent is 4, not 5: `M⁴ > 0` while `(M³)₂₁ = 0` (`Psc.BPrimeCore.zeta_primitive`). The audit note and `tools/audit_bpa_bprime.py` (`primitive_positive_power: 5`) report a true but non-minimal exponent.
  - `β > 1` needs only primitivity on at least two letters, not irreducibility (`Psc.BPrimeCore.one_lt_perron`). Strict dominance `‖z‖ < β` is the Wielandt equality case with the sign `−1` excluded (`norm_lt_perron`).
  - Audit step 3 (letter frequencies) can be replaced by the window-sum exchange `Σ_{k<K}(P(k+t)−P k) = Σ_{j<t}(P(K+j)−P j)` (`Psc.BPrimeCore.window_sum_swap`). This gives `|K·P t − t·P K| ≤ K L + t²` (`avg_bound`). The proof then needs only bounded discrepancy along `tₙ`, not cuts (`bprime_core_of_discrepancy`). For the specimen, the discrepancy is unbounded (`zeta_unbounded_discrepancy`).
  - Recurrence of the fixed point (Sirvent–Solomyak's initial reduction) follows from primitivity of `σ` itself, with no power (`Psc.BPrimeBinding.unbounded_of_primitive`). Neither recurrence nor primitivity can be dropped from "termination gives bounded gaps" (`stairBoundedGaps`, `stair_not_primitive`).
  - The plastic substitution `0 ↦ 1, 1 ↦ 2, 2 ↦ 01` has no one-sided fixed point (`Psc.BPrime.plastic_not_isFixed`), so it cannot be a prefix-seeded B′ control. The positive control is Tribonacci (`trib_control`): every hypothesis except the finiteness of `I(0)` holds, and `IsPisot β` is proved directly. That `bpa-0` terminates there is not checked in Lean.
  - Irreducibility enters steps 2–6 only through `q · w ≠ 0` and the cubic boundary. `reducible_control` (`0 ↦ 0001, 1 ↦ 0111`, `W = 01`, `q = (1, −1)` for the root `2 > 1`) is a counterexample to dropping it in step 4, not to B′, since `01` is not a prefix of the fixed point.
  - **Tooling pitfalls.** A `PscVerif` module outside the import closure of `PscVerif.lean` fails `tests/test_proof_bank.py` (`check_imports`) and is not built by the default `lake build`. `lake env lean` on such a module alone is not a receipt. The notation `ᵥ*` needs `open Matrix`; without it Lean reports "elaboration function for `Mathlib.Tactic.subscriptTerm` has not been implemented".

  Locator: [general-structure note §3](c4-general-structure-2026-11-06.md); [B′ audit](c4-bprime-independent-proof-audit-2026-11-07.md); `proof/PscVerif/PscVerif/{BPrimeCore,BPrimeBinding,BPrime}.lean`.

- **2026-11-09 — Review round on `PscVerif.BPrime`: Fibonacci's `bpa-0` terminates (checked in Lean), and B′ is stated over the literal recursion (3.1).** This updates the entry above, whose counts and "open" list are now stale. The review found no blocker or major issue. Its minor findings were applied as follows.
  - **Literal (3.1).** `Psc.BPrimeBinding.IW_eq_iUnion_ssLevel` proves `⋃ₙ ssLevel s u W n = IW s u W` (fixed point, prefix seed, primitive). `Psc.BPrime.bprime_ss`, `corB_infinite_ss` and `corB_infinite_cubic_ss` state termination on Sirvent–Solomyak's recursion read literally. `zetaU_ssLevel_infinite` instantiates the last on the audit specimen.

Over all zero returns `I₀(0) ⊆ {(0, 0), (01, 10)}` (`fib_level0_subset`), and `I(0)` lies in the four states of `Psc.Descendants.fib_bpaStates`. The key fact is that `11` is not a factor of the fixed point (`fibU_not_one_one`). At shift 1, a block is `0 1^{m−1}` read against its shift (`equiv1_win_succ_iff`, `exists_win_of_mem_blocks`), so `m ≤ 2`. Over all of `I₀(0)`, a parent of `(1, 1)` is a coincidence block (`fib_one_one_parents_I0`).
  - **Non-vacuity where B′ has content.** `Psc.BPrime.fib_control` discharges every hypothesis of `bprime` at once on two letters: `χ_M = X² − X − 1` is irreducible, and `I(0)` is finite. `fib_bprime` derives `‖1 − β‖ ≤ 1` from termination, and `fib_isPisotOrSalem` derives the Pisot-or-Salem reading. On three letters no irreducible specimen has a checked finite `I(W)`; Tribonacci termination is still not checked.
  - **DRY.** One rational-root lemma, `Psc.BPrimeCore.irreducible_cubic`, replaces the two copied scripts in `zeta_irreducible` and `trib_irreducible`. Degree 3 now comes from `Psc.Degree4Floor.natDegree_charpoly`. The Tribonacci modulus now comes from `exists_other_roots` and `det_eq_mul_roots` (`det M = 1`), so `trib_prod_roots` is gone. One fixed-point constructor, `Psc.BPrimeBinding.seedLimit`, now serves `fibU`, `zetaU` and `tribU`. `exists_isFixed_of_seed` needs only a nonerasing `σ`.
  - **Coverage.** Every form of Corollary B (i) is now instantiated on the specimen: `zetaU_IW_infinite_of_root` (`corB_infinite`) and `zetaU_IW_infinite_of_not_isPisotOrSalem`. The helper declarations have docstrings.
  - **Not done.** The status prose in [general-structure §7](c4-general-structure-2026-11-06.md) and the audit's acceptance matrix still predate the Lean composition. The review recommends updating them after a hosted CI run on the exact head, and there is none yet. The inline Pisot existentials in `Degree4Floor`, `Degree3Sector`, `ReductioControls` and `StrictSystem` are not restated through `IsPisot`.
  - **Receipts (local only).** `lake build PscVerif` passes with 0 warnings. `ProofBankAudit.lean` reports 3,950 declarations and 3,037 theorems, standard axioms only. The proof bank has 1,823 named proofs, and `tests/test_proof_bank.py` passes.
  - **Tooling pitfalls.** `decide` gets stuck on `¬ IsSquare (5 : ℚ)` at `Rat.sqrt`, but `decide +kernel` proves it. With `h : z = α`, `rw [h]` also rewrites the `z` inside `starRingEnd ℂ z`, so rewrite the conjugate's equation first. A `set m := …` variable and its unfolded body are different atoms to `omega`, so rewrite with `← hm` first.

  Locator: `proof/PscVerif/PscVerif/{BPrimeCore,BPrimeBinding,BPrime}.lean`.

- **2026-11-09 — B′ integration: the Lean proof of Proposition B′ / Corollary B (i) is written up with Work shown, and the acceptance matrix is mapped item by item.** This entry adds the integration round to the two B′ entries above. The review-round entry's counts are current, and the first entry's are stale: 1,823 named proofs; the audit reports 3,950 declarations and 3,037 theorems with standard axioms only; `lake build` passes with 0 warnings. Hosted exact-head CI still does not exist. Findings worth not recomputing:
  - **The frequency-free route, in one line.** From `|D_t(·)| ≤ L`, the window-sum exchange gives `|K·P(t) − t·P(K)| ≤ K L + t²`. Choosing `K = t_m`, another substitution level, makes `P(K) = M^m w` exact. A left eigenvector then gives `‖t_m zⁿ q(w) − tₙ z^m q(w)‖ ≤ (t_m L + tₙ²)‖q‖₁`. Dividing by `t_m ≥ c β^m` and letting `m → ∞` gives `‖z‖ⁿ |q(w)| ≤ L ‖q‖₁`. Strict dominance `‖z‖ < β` replaces the audit's `q(f) = 0`, and primitivity, not just irreducibility of the nonnegative matrix, is what pays for it. Letter frequencies, Cesàro limits and Galois-normalized eigenvectors are never constructed.
  - **Hypotheses.** Lean controls show that two steps cannot drop a hypothesis: irreducibility in the nonvanishing step (`reducible_control`) and recurrence in "termination gives cut gaps" (`stairBoundedGaps`). Both refute the weakened step, not B′. `degree_three_needed` shows only that the abstract cubic-boundary lemma `norm_ne_one_of_cubic` needs its degree hypothesis among arbitrary monic irreducible rational polynomials. `X² + 1` is not the `χ_M` of a primitive substitution. The strict disc is formalized only for `natDegree χ_M = 3`. In degree two Lean has only the closed disc. The strict disc follows there by hand, since the conjugate `tr M − β` is real and `±1` would be a rational root, but it is not stated in Lean. In degree `≥ 4` Lean has only the closed disc, and nothing is known here or claimed about the strict disc there. `plastic_not_isFixed` is a scope remark: the plastic substitution has no one-sided fixed point, so B′ does not apply to it. It does not show that the fixed point is necessary. Shown unnecessary: nonerasing as a separate hypothesis, uniform recurrence beyond primitivity of `σ` itself, diagonalizability, integrality in the cubic boundary, and cuts inside the core, where a bounded discrepancy along `tₙ` suffices (`bprime_core_of_discrepancy`).
  - **Controls.** Negative: (a) the audit specimen at its fixed point, every form of Corollary B (i) (`zetaU_IW_infinite`, `zetaU_ssLevel_infinite`); (b) reducibility. Positive, for non-vacuity: one letter, the period-two specimen (`per_control`; its `χ_M = X(X − 2)` is reducible by hand, not a conjunct in Lean), Fibonacci with a checked finite `I(0)` (`fib_control`), and Tribonacci without its termination check (`trib_control`). The Python script's swap seed `(01, 10)` is a block of `I₀(0)` for the specimen's fixed point `σ^ω(0) = 012000101012…`, at positions `[5,7)` and `[7,9)` of the reduction of `(u, S¹u)`: `u[0,k)` and `u[1,k+1)` have equal Parikh vectors at `k = 5, 7, 9` and unequal ones at `k = 6, 8` (by hand, not in Lean). `I(W)` is child-closed (`Psc.BPrimeBinding.IW_childClosed`), so the script's swap closure is a sub-closure of `I(0)`. Its 12-generation growth is therefore a bounded non-proof regression for both (i) at `W = 0` and (iii). `zetaU_IW_infinite` remains the proof of (i).
  - **Withdrawn before commit (review).** An earlier draft of this entry and of the note listed "degree three for the strict disc" (`degree_three_needed`) and "a one-sided fixed point" (`plastic_not_isFixed`) as hypotheses shown necessary by Lean controls. Neither control shows that. It also called the swap seed "a Corollary B (iii) seed, not a regression of (i)", which is inaccurate. The note, this entry and the module docstrings of `BPrimeCore` and `BPrime` were reworded. **Pitfall:** a counterexample to an abstract lemma (`X² + 1`) is a control of that lemma, not of the theorem that uses it. Check that the witness is realizable inside the theorem's hypotheses (here: as the `χ_M` of a primitive substitution).
  - **Proof bank.** `claim_governance.toml` has no B′ claim. `BPATerminationInterface` is the Pisot-side literature interface "PDS ⇔ termination with coincidence", so binding B′ to it would misstate both. No `lean_support` binding was added and no claim was invented. The regenerated bank is unchanged by the docs.
  - **Tooling pitfalls.**
    - A new note in `docs/` needs a `**Status:**` line within its first 10 lines. Otherwise every bold `**Proposition …**` / `**Corollary …**` lead-in needs a status label within 6 lines (the `claims` check).
    - Avoid "is proved", "is established" and "settles" within 120 characters of an open alias (`PSC`, `G1`, "finite BPA") unless a negation is nearby (the `promotion` check).
    - `tools/make_proof_bank.py` inventories statement openings in `docs/*.md` by line number. A line inserted above an opening drifts the bank until it is regenerated. Here no opening follows the appended status lines, so `--check` stayed green.

  Locator: [B′ Lean note](c4-bprime-lean-2026-11-09.md); [general-structure note §3](c4-general-structure-2026-11-06.md); [priority note](c4-bprime-machine-verification-priority-2026-11-07.md).

- **2026-11-09 — Quantifier audit, part I: three readings of "productive", one Lean interface, and the finiteness premise three prose arrows had dropped.** First item of the 2026-11-09 priority addendum of `README.llm.txt`.
  - **The readings.** (E) some path reaches a coincidence; (AE) every reachable state does; (AE°) every reachable cyclic state does (the form of SCC Producer and of BH); (A) every admissible path does. `Psc.StrictSystem.Productive` is (E) and `TerminatesWithCoincidence` is "finite reachable set and (AE)", both by `Iff.rfl` (`Psc.QuantifierBinding`). The literature criterion is (AE): Martensen, arXiv:math/0309194, Theorem 3.10 ("every … pair in `I(w, L)` leads to a coincidence"); for a finite Markov chain probability one is (AE) (Katoen's lecture notes, "almost sure reachability theorem").
  - **(A) is false inside the standing class.** Fibonacci from `(01, 10)` terminates with coincidence and has the non-coincident cycle `(01, 10) → (10, 01) → (01, 10)` (`fib_lasso`). Do not state or test an interface of the form "every path meets a coincidence".
  - **Finiteness is a separate input in both directions.** Exclusion of finite closed strict components gives nothing without it (`ray_exclusion_vacuous`, `loopRay_recurrent_not_closed`), and neither does productivity of every cyclic state (`ray_cyclicProductive_vacuous`). The abstract graphs are not claimed to be realised by a substitution.
  - **Prose surfaces that printed `C4 ⇒ C3-local ⇒ C2 ⇒ SCC Producer` without G1**: `docs/proof-ladder.md`, `docs/conjecture-ledger.md` §E, `docs/c4-finite-domain-closure-2026-11-05.md` §1. Each now carries a dated correction. The manuscript (`prop:chain`, `thm:sink`), Lean (`hfin`) and the generated ledger had the premise all along.
  - **Null is not empty, and null needs conservation.** With a positive mass conserved at rate `θ`, strict mass `o(θⁿ)` gives (AE) (`MassGraph.hereditarilyProductive_of_nullStrict`); it never gives (A) (`lassoMass`), and without conservation it gives nothing (`slow`). Akiyama–Lee, arXiv:1003.2898, Theorem 4.1 has the same shape: the residual graph is nonempty in general and cannot contain a *real* overlap.
  - **Literature-settled specimens (not used).** Akiyama–Lee report no overlap coincidence for `0 ↦ 03, 1 ↦ 0, 2 ↦ 21, 3 ↦ 2` and for `0 ↦ 051000, 1 ↦ 324100, 2 ↦ 24100, 3 ↦ 324333, 4 ↦ 051433, 5 ↦ 51433`. Both are primitive with reducible characteristic polynomials, `(x² − x − 1)(x² − x + 1)` and `(x − 1)²(x² − 6x + 1)(x² − 4x + 1)` (computed for the stop/go note; the second has two eigenvalues outside the unit circle). They are outside the standing class by irreducibility and are on four and six letters, so the alphabet-3 kernels do not apply.
  - **Tooling pitfalls.** `deriving Fintype` on a three-constructor inductive failed in this toolchain (`enumList_nodup` type mismatch); write the instance by hand. `push_neg` is deprecated in favour of `push Not`. A `Prop`-valued structure field such as `target a := a = true` is not unfolded by `decide`; state the unfolded proposition with `show` or a typed binder.

  Locator: [audit, part I](quantifier-transfer-audit-2026-11-09.md); [stop/go](quantifier-transfer-audit-stop-go-2026-11-09.md); `proof/PscVerif/PscVerif/{QuantifierInterface,QuantifierBinding}.lean`; `kernel/tests/test_quantifier_controls.mojo`.

So `MCLedgerG1AndProducer` and `MCLedgerG1AndC4` listed `PDS` as reachable while asserting `StandardBPAEquivalenceNotEstablished`. `tools/make_ledger.py` now gives `PDS` that dependency and the two models assume it. No status changes. Local receipts: `make_ledger.py --check`; `tests/test_ledger_generation.py`; `proof/tla/check.sh` with `tla2tools.jar` v1.8.0, 18 models pass.
  - **Tooling pitfall.** `proof/tla/check.sh` leaves `MCNonProductive_TTrace_*.{bin,tla}` in `proof/tla/` (the expected violation writes a trace). Delete them before `git add -A`.
