- **2026-11-16 — property (F) for Tribonacci `01/02/0` is machine-checked in Lean; with it Theorem FC1 has its first complete machine-checked instance on three letters.** Module `PscVerif.GeometricFTriple` (no `sorry`, no `native_decide`; axioms `propext`, `Classical.choice`, `Quot.sound`): `trib_propertyF` for every positive left eigen-row, `trib_fc1_hypotheses_complete`, `trib_fc1` (`fc1_pip3` with no hypothesis left), `trib_fc1_evaluated` (no strict system; every pair of letters left strongly coincident at level 1; `g`-periodic letters `{0}`); negative control `01/12/021` fails (F) (`ctrl_not_propertyF`, the `f`-fixed state `(2; −1, 0, 1)`). The `ℓ¹` trap of Lemma FM-T (family note) does not apply to Tribonacci (the coefficients `β − 1` and `θ` of the contracting factor sum to about 1.38 > 1); an explicit quadratic Lyapunov form `Q = u₁² + (β − 1)u₁u₂ + θu₂²` with `Q′ ≤ (4/3)θQ + 1` confines cycles to `v₁ ∈ [−2, 2]`, `v₂ ∈ [−2, 3]`, and 30 radix chains (through 32 nonzero pairs, at most 7 steps) reach the vertex state. No novelty is claimed for (F) on Tribonacci (finiteness of the Tribonacci number is classical); the instance adds no fact about Tribonacci's strict systems (`trib_no_strictSystem` is on main). Checked by incremental build and a module-restricted axiom audit only; the isolated-checker receipt must come from CI. Not in Lean: (F) for any other three-letter substitution or family; PIP3 membership of `01/12/021`. Locators: `proof/PscVerif/PscVerif/GeometricFTriple.lean`; [round-3 note §3](c4-round3-2026-11-16.md).

- **2026-11-16 — Theorem Fork holds for every determinant at `dim H¹ = 3` (written proof, one independent adversarial verifier, no refutation).** For `σ ∈ PIP3` with any `det M` and `dim H¹ = 3`, every pair `(T, T′)` of a strict component `Z` has a second partner `T″` of `T`, with `(T, T″) ∈ Z`, such that `T′` and `T″` share no tile at any level; `π₁: Z → Ω_σ` is injective at no point; the cut sets are disjoint; and the interior periodic address is realised by a partner in the fibre of `h_φ` over the identity of the solenoid `𝕋_A`. The determinant enters no step: the solenoid is used only through the fibre relation of `h_φ` (same fibres as `g_max`, from BBK Thm 5.1 and BKS §2.5), discreteness of fibre-mates (coordinate `p₀` only) and the identity fibre; Lemma 9″ is BKS Cor. 3 and Thm 5 (not new), Lemma 10 and (Z4) at every determinant are on main (rank-two note §8, §6.1), and `cr ≥ 3` is Corollary N+. Binding corrections of the verifier: the BKS locator is §2.5; Lemma I (`≈_s` is the same for `Φ`, its powers and the proper version) holds modulo the proper-version construction, whose rewriting references were not read; BBK Thm 5.1 rests on Barge–Kwapisz 2006 Lemma 13.1 (read) and on Remark 1 of BBK's reference [18] (not read); the scratch alignment verdicts on the reducible controls are relative to the return group of legal words. Where it stops: every reducible strict control at irreducibility (Lemma 1(1); (Z4) fails, scratch); Thue–Morse at Lemma 9″, where Lemma 10 and the conclusion of Fork are both false, so the stop is sharp for the method; the Barge–Bruin–Jones–Sadun triple covers (literature) are consistent with Lemma 10. A necessary condition, vacuous on every computed domain; not machine-checked; human review pending. For nonunit `σ` with `dim H¹ = 3` and a strict component, the unique-partner premise of the retired derived-substitution route is false, not merely unproved. Locators: [round-3 note §4](c4-round3-2026-11-16.md); [closure-attempt note §16](c4-closure-attempt-2026-11-16.md).

- **2026-11-16 — the unit case with first-defect weights `(m, m, m)` of the rank-two wall is an obstruction (not discharged): no available constraint separates a literal strict component from an abstract child system; do not re-run the three directions as posed.** Setting: `σ ∈ PIP3` unit, `cr = 2`, a strict system, first defect degree `r = 3m`, every first-defect eigenvalue `ε = (det M)^m = ±1`. First failed premise: an abstract child system (involution `J`, `J`-equivariant primitive `Σ`, maps `Pk`, `D_r` with `Pk N = M Pk`, `Pk J = Pk`, `D_r J = −D_r`, `D_r N = ε D_r`, `ρ(N₋) < β`) meets every round-2 tool (FP, TT, NI, W as matrix statements, U, (L0), the sieve, S+, P+, H3) and the three new inputs tried: (i) an `ε`-defect cocycle with a bounded / unbounded dichotomy (Proposition E: bounded iff a Barge–Diamond `G_0` coboundary; Barge's eigenvalue 1 of `X − Y` is itself such a coboundary, so neither branch contradicts Lemma S+); (ii) the explicit degree-three structure (Proposition R3: `Θ(K₃(T))` has irreducible characteristic polynomial in `ℚ[M]₀`); (iii) a swap-Lefschetz identity (Lemma L±), satisfied by every swap-equivariant abstract `Σ`. An explicit two-branch model on the corpus specimen `1/2/021` (exact scratch, except a numeric Pisot screen) realises both branches. The missing ingredient is literal factorization, where the concentration note §6 already stops. Propositions E and R3 and Lemma L± are single-prover drafts with no verifier and are not dependencies. A candidate new input, not a result: a positivity statement for the first defect on three letters, the analogue of the sign of the signed area on two letters. Locators: [round-3 note §5](c4-round3-2026-11-16.md); [concentration note §6](c4-concentration-attack-2026-11-06.md).

- **2026-11-16 — exploratory scratch observations of the unit `(m, m, m)` route (not replayable from the repository; do not quote as evidence).** (i) Irreducible pairs with `K₂ = 0`, by length 7–13, as counted by the scratch enumeration (length 7: the six classes of the concentration note): 6, 24, 198, 1,230, 7,644, 50,406, 323,274; those whose `Θ(K₃)` has irreducible characteristic polynomial: 0, 0, 0, 36, 492, 7,536, 81,636; pairs with `K₃ = 0` first occur at length 12 (12 pairs), consistent with the concentration note §4. (ii) The pinned pair `T13` also puts `K₃` in the det-eigenspace of the unit Pisot matrices `−3I − 7M0 + 51M0²` (`det −1`) and `−141I − 357M0 + 2245M0²` (`det +1`) of `ℤ[M0]`, so weight `(1, 1, 1)` with eigenvalue `±1` is met at matrix level by a single pair for a unit PIP matrix; no substitution is claimed. (iii) An exhaustive scan of the 59,319 substitutions with images of length at most 3 (caps 400 states, length 300; 8,748 capped, reported as capped) finds 432 closed strict sinks on 276 substitutions; none has its whole first-defect image on the unit circle, and the 48 primitive swap-symmetric sinks all have singular `M` and first-defect eigenvalue 0. (iv) No irreducible `K₂ = 0` pair of length at most 13 has `K₃` in the det-sector of any of the 72 incidence matrices of the σ-side specimens. The σ-side count itself is pinned: 264 odd-determinant corpus specimens (cells 26 and 30 of the cell table of `test_rank_two_parity.mojo`) satisfy every recorded necessary condition of `cr = 2`. Locator: [round-3 note §5, §7](c4-round3-2026-11-16.md).

- **2026-11-16 — tooling pitfalls from round 3 of the C4 closure attempt.** (i) `psc.rank_two_parity.graph_map_on` refuses a disconnected eventual range: there `H⁰ ≠ ℚ` (Barge–Diamond `G_0 ≠ 0`) and the cycle-type formula for `c_n` is false; every reducible control of the brief, `1/012/1` and the two-letter `1/010` are refused this way, and `divisor_profile` refuses every non-PIP3 control by name. (ii) Lemma N of the module makes `n = 1, …, 42` exact for "every admissible `n`"; scanning further adds nothing. (iii) A new `require_contract` is credited by the coverage audit only once its receipt is in `kernel/build/claim-receipts.tsv` (collected by `kernel/run_tests.sh` or CI); a single-test run collects nothing, and the implementer's audit then reported "declared, but the recorded run did not reach it" (the receipt was collected before the commit). (iv) Lean: `GeometricFPisot.periodic_bound` and `periodicStates_finite` cannot be used to enumerate periodic states (their eigenfunctionals are chosen by `Classical.choice`); an explicit form is needed. (v) Lean: the `ℓ¹` trap of Lemma FM-T needs the coefficient condition of the family note and fails for Tribonacci; check it before reusing the trap on another specimen. (vi) The scratch Python prototype of the divisor census used floating-point root checks for its Pisot screen and the unit route's positivity screen is numeric: both are exploratory only, and the canonical figures are the Mojo ones. (vii) Alignment of strict first pairs (scratch, Fork controls) was computed against the return group of legal words, not BBK's `Θ(i)` over the full strand space. Locators: `kernel/psc/rank_two_parity.mojo`; `kernel/tests/test_rank_two_parity.mojo`; `proof/PscVerif/PscVerif/GeometricFTriple.lean`; [round-3 note §7](c4-round3-2026-11-16.md).

- **2026-11-17 — a single reference in the legal zero fibre gives an equivalent PSC target.** The written reduction uses BBK generic fibres and compactness to place each legal strand in a maximal pairwise noncoincident family. At the solenoid identity every legal strand is inflation-periodic. The complete seed catalogue consists of periodic legal seams and all based periodic interior cuts. A reference seam must meet each seed by two literal occurrences at equal depth. The unit and determinant-two controls show that seam-only catalogues omit legal strands off (F) and that coincidence can occur away from the origin even when the two cuts never have the same type. The independent literal expansion in the Mojo regression checks the reported periods and first depths. The reduction and universal occurrence obligation are distinct. The universal obligation remains pending. Locator: [zero-fibre proof and replay](psc-zero-fibre-coincidence-2026-11-17.md).

- **2026-11-17 — literal zero-fibre overlap paths and a bounded closed-set criterion checked in Lean.** `PscVerif.ZeroFibreOverlap` uses the actual parent and local occurrence from `locateSubst`, proves projected descendant containment, and identifies common patch edges with strict overlap paths to the first diagonal. The existing `ContractionData` gives finiteness and a root-independent bound for a diagonal-free child-closed set. The latter uses a minimum stable size in the finite closed set and a child selected at the larger left endpoint. BBK Remark 4.3 with the full return lattice makes exclusion of such sets equivalent to rank one. Universal exclusion, C4 and PSC remain open. Locator: [literal overlap proof and replay](psc-zero-fibre-coincidence-2026-11-17.md).

- **2026-11-17 — each literal closed overlap phase has centred displacement and a lattice support bound.** The written proof compares the two primitive supertile frequency limits and uses stable contraction to erase starting positions. A diagonal-free phase has both signs in every linear direction. The spatial overlay walk gives unit-step connectivity, even with aligned starts, since simultaneous boundary events return the offset to zero. Each ternary phase needs seven distinct offsets, or eight if it omits zero. Lean checks the seven-offset lattice bound from the sign and connectivity inputs. The Mojo replay checks the sharp abstract supports and the depth-two exit from the open `000122/01/0` self-loop. Universal exclusion and PSC remain open. Locator: [phase displacement proof and replay](overlap-phase-displacement-2026-11-17.md).

- **2026-11-17 — full periodic fibres larger than two have a sub-Perron count at rank two.** A finite literal triple overlap graph loses width from every sink, because a full-width sink realizes three pairwise noncoincident legal strands. A path and the bounded return displacement of its first centre tile recover the entire base class via `M^n-I`, even when the fibre permutation has no fixed lifts. Two-lift fibres fixing both lifts and those exchanging the lifts each have leading count half the toral total. The resulting pair capacity has the same leading rate as strict return demand. A cofactor above the triple graph rate would force a shortage, while the unit neutral sector still needs a literal compatibility bound. Locator: [periodic fibre bound](c4-periodic-fibre-bound-2026-11-17.md).

- **2026-11-17 — at unit rank two the shared-cut gap test keeps all ordinary noncoincident periodic pairs if a strict system exists and keeps none otherwise.** Uniform separation of distinct cuts within a fibre and uniqueness of the minimal joining give the forward implication. A single bounded-gap noncoincident pair gives a finite closed system of literal irreducible factors. Bounded backward ancestry puts every legal same-fibre overlap in the existing formal box, so the full box alignment verdict decides the gap condition. The scaled centre-tile catalogue counts all lifts in a specified nonzero periodic fibre and tests every pair. Named sizes are four, five and three with four, one and zero ordinary fixed lifts. No strict system or shortage in the neutral sector is established. Locator: [shared cuts, full fibres and replay](c4-rank-two-periodic-compatibility-2026-11-17.md).

- **2026-11-17 — strict periodic compatibility is membership in the aligned overlap sink.** An aligned nonproductive sink reconstructs finite strict factors from periodic first-letter rays. At unit rank two any strict joining has that unique sink, and a periodic pair with two interior tiles belongs to the joining precisely when its central state lies there. Ordered occurrence trace counts subtract the original physical endpoint walks. The signed bigon and overlap traces agree at every power because an exchanged return cannot have a one-track seam. Ordinary traces differ by the finite one-track seam correction. The independent twice-power literal scan replays 20 cases, including nonzero exchanges and zero-class interior lifts. The two documented abstract orders have zero ordinary capacity at their required power two. Uniform exclusion of actual neutral sinks remains open. Locator: [literal overlap return proof and replay](c4-periodic-overlap-returns-2026-11-17.md).

- **2026-11-17 — no degree-two area functional has a sign law on literal irreducible noncoincident balanced pairs on three letters, in any tested form; do not retry sign laws on single pairs.** With the canonical stable form `A^s = det(r_β, ·, ·)(K₂) = r_β·⋆K₂` (the left eigen-row of `Λ²M` for `det M/β`; the pairing with `η₂∧η₃` has no canonical sign, and relabelling multiplies `A^s` by the sign of the permutation): the hub-on-top law fails at length 2 on every specimen and every hub (`A^s(ab, ba) = det(r_β, e_a, e_b)`, positive iff `b ≡ a + 1 (mod 3)`); the cyclic first-letter, last-letter and rational-shadow laws fail at length 3 (`001/012/01`, legal `(201, 012)`: `A^s = 1 + 2β − β² < 0`; Tribonacci `(0201, 1020)`: `A^s = 2 − β² < 0`; `1/02/202`, `(021, 120)`: cyclic sum `−1` while `A^s > 0`); a sign that is a function of the first pair or of (first, last) fails per specimen (Tribonacci `(01, 10)` positive, `(0201, 1020)` negative); the `E^u`-plane functionals fail (`1/021/12`); and `A^s` has mixed signs on the actual strict systems of C-D1, C-D2, `10/102/211` and `01/201/112`. Exact sign decisions, exploratory scratch (not pinned); the cited values were re-checked with exact algebraic numbers. Single prover, no verifier. Locator: [round-4 note §§2.1–2.3](c4-round4-2026-11-17.md).

- **2026-11-17 — where a sign law for a degree-two area functional holds, it excludes no strict system without coherence of the orientation along child edges, which is literal factorization again; at `cr = 2`, W3 already gives what Perron–Frobenius gives.** Proposition D (single-prover written proof, no verifier): on a strongly connected strict system of a primitive `σ` with `ρ(N) = β`, real `|λ| < β` and `A N = λA`, `A ≢ 0`, every orientation `s` with `sA ≥ 0` has both preserving and reversing child edges, so a sign law relative to `s` says only that `sA` is a positive left eigenvector of the signed matrix at `λ`. Such laws hold and exclude nothing on `1/012/101` (six-state strict system; the cyclic first-letter law holds on every state; `x = (1, √2, √2 − 1)` with eigenvalue `√2 − 1`; 6 preserving and 8 reversing edges), on two letters (positivity holds, Proposition 2L, but under `100/01` the pair `(01, 10)` has the children `(10, 01)` and `(001, 100)` of opposite orientation), and on the abstract model of Obstruction U3 (`K₂ ≡ 0`; the degree-three Perron functional is sign-coherent, `f(A − C) = f`, with a reversing edge). Correction of emphasis to round-3 note §5.6: the two-letter sign, with the eigen-relation, does not by itself reproduce the two-letter coincidence theorem. Locator: [round-4 note §§2.2, 2.4, 2.5](c4-round4-2026-11-17.md).

- **2026-11-17 — exploratory scratch observations of the positivity route (not pinned; do not quote as evidence).** (i) Ordered balanced-pair-algorithm closures from the six seeds `(ab, ba)` over the 4,554 corpus specimens (cap 4,000 states per specimen, never reached): 771,852 states, 48 of them with `K₂ = 0` (the 12 specimens of the `011/2/120` class, lengths 9 and 19); the cyclic first-letter law fails on 337,848 states; the sign of `A^s` is a function of the first pair on 186 specimens and of (first, last) on 576. (ii) The 648 corpus specimens with three real roots (pinned count, `test_c4_fc3_family.mojo`) all have `det M = −1`; with the pinned 648 specimens of determinant `−1` (`test_h1_census.mojo`) the two sets are then equal, and that identity is scratch. (iii) A bounded search finds all three letter pairs left strongly coincident on 4,542 corpus specimens (the 12 plastic-number specimens need more levels), so a hub orientation of strict states is vacuous on the corpus. Locator: [round-4 note §2.7](c4-round4-2026-11-17.md).

- **2026-11-17 — at `dim H¹ = 3` and every determinant, the top-track map `π₁` of a strict component is not a covering, and is locally injective at no point, whenever the component `𝒦` of the zero-fibre class graph is a tree, in particular when `g` and `g_R` are both injective (written proof, one independent adversarial verifier, no refutation; not machine-checked).** Lemma C: `π₁` is a covering iff it is locally injective iff no tiling has two distinct partners that agree on a half-line, and otherwise it is locally injective nowhere. Theorem NC′ (any `dim H¹`): a covering forces a cycle in `𝒦`, i.e. a Barge 2015 asymptotic cycle among members of `h_φ⁻¹(0)`. Corollary NC: under (NM_V) or a tree `𝒦`, at `dim H¹ = 3`, some tiling has two distinct strict partners that agree on a half-line. Vacuous on every computed domain; the injective class is empty in the corpus (the 90 specimens with `dim H¹ = 3` and two periodic letters on both sides split 30/30/30/0 by injectivity of `g`, `g_R`; scratch) and nonempty beyond it (24 specimens with images of length at most 4, e.g. `010/102/2001`; scratch). Sharp controls: Thue–Morse and `012/120/201` (`ER = K_{2,2}`, `K_{3,3}`; `π₁` a homeomorphism). Binding verifier corrections: the cycle notion is Barge 2015's, not a new invariant; Fibonacci, where `𝒦` has a cycle at `l = 0`, is already on main. Locators: [round-4 note §3](c4-round4-2026-11-17.md); [closure-attempt note §17](c4-closure-attempt-2026-11-16.md).

- **2026-11-17 — the round-1 covering-degree parity draft now has a complete written proof (one verifier, no refutation); it is not a sharpening.** If `π₁` is a `k`-fold covering at `dim H¹ = 3`: `k·𝔉(Z) ⊂ E = ⋃ β^{−n}(ℤr₀ + ℤr₁ + ℤr₂)` (Proposition F, through the norm map and Lemma 9″), `2 ≤ k ≤ cr − 1`, and for swap-closed `Z`, `k/2 ∈ ℤ[1/det M]`, i.e. `k` is even unless `det M` is even (Corollary P). The prover's "sharper than the draft" was corrected by the verifier: for an integer `k` the condition is the draft's. Unique ergodicity of `Z` is used without proof (a fixable gap, from the proof of Lemma 8). On the tree class of Corollary NC the statement is vacuous. Locators: [round-4 note §§3.2–3.4](c4-round4-2026-11-17.md); [closure-attempt note §11](c4-closure-attempt-2026-11-16.md) (the draft's row).

- **2026-11-17 — the first failed premise of the covering question in general is "`𝒦` is a tree"; exploratory scratch finds a tree on every specimen computed (not pinned).** When `g` or `g_R` is not injective, the zero fibre can contain inflation-periodic asymptotic pairs that branch away from `0`. Scratch: on the 90 corpus specimens above, exact merge certificates exist on 24 (12 right, all with `g_R` non-injective; 12 left, all with `g` non-injective) and `𝒦` is a tree on all 90 (24 with one pendant interior member); beyond the corpus, 271 sampled specimens (prover), the exhaustive shell of image length exactly 4 (3,270 specimens) and 748 seeded-sample specimens (verifier) are all trees, apart from capped runs reported as capped. Interior members were enumerated with 60-digit or float-sized comparisons, so the absence of further members is not exact. Locator: [round-4 note §§3.5, 3.7](c4-round4-2026-11-17.md).

- **2026-11-17 — Lemmas 1(1), 1(3) and 4 of the (F)-class note are machine-checked in Lean; Lemma 5 is not.** Module `PscVerif.GeometricFLemmas` (67 declarations, no `sorry`, no `native_decide`; axioms `propext`, `Classical.choice`, `Quot.sound`): Lemma 1(1) on any finite alphabet for any nonzero real left eigen-row (`eigenrow_eq_zero_of_irreducible`, `lenHom_eq_zero_iff`), which discharges the hypothesis `hzero` of `propertyF_reverse_iff` and `fc2_rank_transfer`; Lemma 1(3) (`eigenvalue_ne_zero_and_pow_ne_one`, `isUnit_incQ_pow_sub_one`); Lemma 4(2) (`propertyF_power_iff`; `m ≥ 1` is needed, `propertyF_power_zero_iff`); Lemma 4(3) (`propertyF_reverse_iff_of_irreducible`, with the state correspondence `(j, v) ↦ (j, e_j − v)`); Lemma 4(1) (`periodic_iff_isInteriorAddress`, `propertyF_iff_no_interiorAddress`). With them (F) holds in Lean for every power of Tribonacci and for its reversal. Checked by incremental build and a module-restricted axiom audit only; the isolated-checker receipt must come from CI. Locators: `proof/PscVerif/PscVerif/GeometricFLemmas.lean`; [round-4 note §4](c4-round4-2026-11-17.md).

- **2026-11-17 — Lemma 4(1) of the (F)-class note, as worded, leaves two hypotheses of its forward direction implicit.** It needs `k ≥ 1` (at `k = 0`, `P = Q = ε` and `(M⁰ − I)v = 0 = p(ε)`), and its step "`ℓ·p(P) = (β^k − 1)ℓ·v > 0`" uses Lemma 1(1), which cannot be dropped: on the reducible strict control `10/102/211` the state `(1, (2, 0, −1))` is `f`-fixed with `v ≠ 0`, yet `σ(1) = 102` has no factorisation `P 1 Q` with `P` nonempty (`carrier_periodic_not_interior`). On PIP3, Lemma 1(1) holds and periods are positive, so no conclusion drawn from the lemma changes. Locators: [round-4 note §4.2](c4-round4-2026-11-17.md); `proof/PscVerif/PscVerif/GeometricFLemmas.lean`.

- **2026-11-17 — corrections by round 4 to sentences of 2026-11-16.** (a) "Whether `π₁` is a covering" is open (closure-attempt note §§3, 10, 15.3, 16.3, 16.4; round-3 note §7; (F)-class note §7): decided negatively on the class of Corollary NC, open outside it; "`π₁` is not a covering map" stays rejected as a universal statement. (b) The round-1 covering-degree parity draft ("not verified; not used", closure-attempt note §11) has a complete written proof with one verifier. (c) "A candidate new input, not a result: a positivity statement for the first defect on three letters" (the entry of 2026-11-16 on the unit `(m, m, m)` case; round-3 note §5.6; closure-attempt note §16.4): at degree two it is an obstruction. (d) "Lemmas 1(1), 1(3), 4 and 5 of the (F)-class note are not machine-checked" (closure-attempt note §15.2): now true of Lemma 5 only. The superseded sentences are listed, not edited. - **2026-11-17 — tooling pitfalls from round 4.** (i) Lean: the first incremental build of `PscVerif.GeometricFLemmas` also compiled the not-yet-built `PscVerif.GeometricFRank`, and the session log reported an out-of-memory kill of some process although the build completed; watch memory when a module pulls in an unbuilt dependency. (ii) Positivity: a survey that pickled every balanced-pair-algorithm state of the corpus reached 1.5 GB and was deleted for disk space; a 22 MB replacement survey reproduces its totals. (iii) Covering: two uncapped beyond-corpus class-graph searches were killed (out of memory, timeout) and produced no data; cap the interior box and the time per specimen, and report capped specimens as capped. (iv) The interior members of the zero fibre were enumerated with 60-digit `mpmath` comparisons or a float-sized box, so their completeness is not exact; relations between members were certified exactly by integer strand segments. Locator: [round-4 note §§3.7, 6](c4-round4-2026-11-17.md).

- **2026-11-17 — FC2 needs one rank realisation, not two: on property (F), `ν_L = ν_R` by an integer argument, machine-checked for every alphabet size.** A common integer translation preserves eventual coincidence of edges; the right-anchored edges `I(−e_c, c)` shifted by `e_{a₀}`, `a₀` a shortest letter, are states over `0` in the left-closed convention (no density of `ℓ(ℤ^d)` is needed); absorption under (F) for `σ`, resp. `σ^R`, turns right families into left families and conversely. So on (F) the integer zero-slice maximum exists and `S(σ) = S(σ^R) = ν_L = ν_R ∈ [1, d]` (`fc2_integer_rank_of_reverse`; with `ℓ` injective, (F) for `σ` suffices: `fc2_integer_rank`). FC2's remaining geometric input is one-sided, `cr(σ) = S(σ)` at `q = 0` (BBK Def. 4.1, Remark 4.3, Lemma 6); step (c) of the class note's proof (reflection of strands, `cr(σ^R) = cr(σ)`) and Remark 4.3 at `q = [t]` are no longer used. Off (F) the integer content of step (c) is recovered: with `ℓ` injective and `d ≥ 2`, any zero-slice family of `σ` reflects, after a translation from the dense length group, to one of `σ^R`, so `S(σ) = S(σ^R)` and `hRankRev` follows from `hRank` (`isMaximumSize_stateFamily_reverse_iff`). Corollary E (i) ⟺ (ii) ⟺ (iii) is machine-checked on the standing class (`fc1_right_of_primitive_pisot`). Literature (stop/go, proceed-narrowed): not found in the sources read; (T) is Barge–Kwapisz 2006 (7.3), the small translation is in the proofs of BBK Remark 4.3 and Minervino–Thuswaldner Lemma 9.1; for unit `σ` the rank-one case also follows by composing printed results (tiling ⟸ (F) + SC; tiling ⟺ PDS; PDS ⟹ prefix SC, Akiyama–Lee Cor. 4.5, for `σ` and `σ^R`). Non-vacuity: Tribonacci, every pair right strongly coincident from the transfer, cross-checked by `decide` on words at levels 4, 4, 3; Thue–Morse (not PIP3, `ℓ` not injective) has all four maxima `2`. Counter-calibration: Barge–Diamond's `φ = 121/221` (eigenvalues 3, 1) has `ν_L = 2`, `ν_R = 1`, with length data but no contraction data (`bd_left_two_right_one`, `bd_not_contractionData`). Excludes nothing: `ν_L = 2` on (F) stays open. Isolated checker not run; no ledger claim. Locators: [FC2 orientation note](c4-fc2-orientation-2026-11-17.md); `proof/PscVerif/PscVerif/GeometricFOrientation.lean`.

- **2026-11-17 — the ℤ-kernel premise (injectivity of `v ↦ ℓ·v` on `ℤ^d`) is machine-checked from the standing hypotheses, by a route through contraction data rather than rational independence.** `det M ≠ 0` from irreducibility of `χ_M` over `ℚ` with `d ≥ 2` (else `X ∣ χ_M`); then `ℓ·v = 0` puts the `M`-orbit of `v` in the finite set `{|ℓ·w| ≤ 0, ν(w) ≤ ν(v)}`, periodicity after cancelling `Mⁱ` forces `ν(v) = 0`, and the multiples of `v` in `{|ℓ·w| ≤ 0, ν(w) ≤ 0}` force `v = 0`. Discharges the premise `hzero` of `GeometricFRank.fc2_rank_transfer` and `propertyF_reverse_iff` on the standing class (`lenHom_injective_of_pisot`). Locators: [FC2 orientation note §3](c4-fc2-orientation-2026-11-17.md); `proof/PscVerif/PscVerif/GeometricFOrientation.lean`.

- **2026-11-17 — superseded sentences (dated corrections; the earlier text stands).** (a) [FC2 rank-transfer review](c4-fc2-rank-transfer-review-2026-11-16.md), "The next Lean obligation is to instantiate the two zero-slice hypotheses … for an actual common BBK rank": only the first is needed; the second follows from it whenever `ℓ` is injective on `ℤ^d` (Theorem O (5) of the orientation note, no (F)), and on (F) also from Theorem O (4). (b) [(F)-class note](c4-geometric-f-class-2026-11-16.md) §4.2, correction (i), "Step (c) replaces that reading": step (c) is no longer needed; `cr ≥ ν_R` follows from Remark 4.3 at `q = 0` with the shifted right-anchored edges. (c) The same note §10(a) and the side-notes entry on FC1 in Lean, "Theorems FC2 … not machine-checked": the integer part of FC2 and Corollary E (i) ⟺ (ii) ⟺ (iii) are now machine-checked; the statements about `cr` are not. Locator: [FC2 orientation note §0, §5](c4-fc2-orientation-2026-11-17.md).

- **2026-11-17 — FC2 orientation: three adversarial verifiers, no refutation; exploratory scratch (not replayable; do not quote as evidence).** Binding corrections applied before the commit: the density step is unnecessary for letter families (the shortest-letter shift suffices, the zero slice being left-closed); injectivity of `ℓ` only carries (F) from `σ` to `σ^R`; `det M ≠ 0` is a genuine extra hypothesis of Lemma Z (Thue–Morse `01/10` has length and contraction data with `ρ = 0` but `ℓ·(1, −1) = 0`); without (F) the zero-slice maximum is only a supremum, so FC2 (1) is stated with the bound half of Remark 4.3. - **2026-11-17 — reference clarification after merging `main@84ca377` into the FC2 orientation branch.** Six 2026-11-17 entries from `main` (zero fibre, overlap phase, periodic fibres, shared cuts, periodic compatibility) now precede the FC2 orientation entries. - **2026-11-18 — the seam correction is bounded from the source, can be positive at +1, and drops out of the endpoint branch.** (i) For any strict rank-two carrier, the one-track correction at order d lies between source-only numbers, computed as the minimum and maximum of cycle-weighted H1−H0 over invariant subgraphs of the cyclic bigram graph, one subgraph per interior f-cycle. Over the 2628 unit corpus members the maximum is 1 at +1 and 2 at −1, and 96 members have a positive ceiling at a root their determinant makes relevant. (ii) Negative control: `01/122/201` has det +1, K_{2,3} cyclic bigrams and one interior 4-cycle. Its literal full-box correction is +1 at every fourth root, confirmed by #601's scanner and by #602's literal-word constructor at powers 1–11 (power 12 refuses at the word cap). So "b_1 ≤ 0" is false where ε = +1 is forced. (iii) If the neutral defect has endpoint-coboundary form, a promotion-weighted endpoint functional κ̃ satisfies D_r = κ̃Π and κ̃A_C = εκ̃, so rank D_r ≤ g_{A_C^-}(ε) with no seam term. The column identity is checked symbolically on six literal boxes at both signs; the unweighted functional fails there. Lean (`PscVerif.NeutralRankSeam`, no `sorry`) checks the abstract core of (iii) — promotion equivariance, adjacency, the column identity, D = κ̃Π and the rank bound — and the `01/122/201` witness: its addresses, bigrams and the 4·[4∣n] trace. Everything is conditional on a strict component and pending independent review. No exclusion is proved. A second Lean round adds T6 (A·Π = Π·N from literal flattening) and D·N = εD from the endpoint form (`NeutralRankSeam`); the spectral step of the #601 cycle theorem via injectivity of the ZMod DFT, the per-copy orbit law and the ceiling of Lemma A (`SeamCycleSpectrum`); and, for `01/122/201`, irreducibility, exhaustion of interior addresses at k ≤ 4 and a single interior f-cycle of exact period 4 (`NeutralRankSeamCycle`). The transfer from a strict component, the endpoint form itself and periods above 4 stay unformalized. Locators: [seam ceiling note](c4-neutral-rank-seam-ceiling-2026-11-18.md), `proof/PscVerif/PscVerif/NeutralRankSeam.lean`, `NeutralRankSeamCycle.lean`, `SeamCycleSpectrum.lean`, [evidence](../evidence/seam-ceiling-2026-11-18/README.md).

- **2026-11-18 — unique strict partners are impossible at coincidence rank two (odd determinant), under the round-4 vertex-asymptote condition (NM_V).** Assume (NM_V), which holds when both endpoint maps are injective (round-4 Lemma A); without it, asymptotic periodic tilings can branch away from the origin. Suppose every tiling had exactly one partner in the strict component `Z` of Theorem W. Then `Z` is the graph of a continuous involution commuting with the flow and `Φ`. By (Z3) it sends a periodic vertex tiling to a vertex tiling. Asymptoticity and the connected Barge–Diamond eventual-range graph (Lemma 7(2)) propagate this to all of `ER`. The result is fixed-point-free involutions on its left and right letters, so `|L| = |R| = 2`, `β₁(ER) ≤ 1` and `dim H¹ ≤ 4`, against B15 Theorem 22. With (W4), the first projection `Z → Ω_σ` is therefore never injective for odd determinant under (NM_V); when both endpoint maps are bijective this holds at every determinant: the neutral unit, `(m,m,m)` case also needs multi-partner tilings. The finite counting is machine-checked; the tiling-space steps are written proofs. No strict system is excluded. Locator: [unique-partner exclusion](c4-unique-partner-exclusion-2026-11-18.md), `proof/PscVerif/PscVerif/UniquePartnerLetters.lean`.

- **2026-11-18 — under (NM_V), a strict joining at rank two is not a covering (odd determinant).** Round-4 Lemmas C and L, with (NM_V), make the left and right letters of vertex-type partners into regular simple graphs on `L` and on `R`. Their common degree is `n_v ≥ 1`. On three letters this leaves two cases:
  - `n_v = 1`, with `|L| = |R| = 2`. B15 Theorem 22 excludes this for odd determinant.
  - `n_v = 2`. A tiling's two vertex partners are then forced to be partners of each other. That gives three pairwise non-coincident tilings in one fibre, against rank two, at every determinant.

  The swap-closed `012/120/201` control is a triangle example, of rank at least 3. So for odd determinant under (NM_V) the joining is locally injective nowhere. The regular-graph count and the triangle lemma are in Lean; no strict system is excluded. Locator: [unique-partner note, Theorem UP′](c4-unique-partner-exclusion-2026-11-18.md), `proof/PscVerif/PscVerif/UniquePartnerLetters.lean`.

- **2026-11-18 — (NM_V) is decided exactly on the corpus, and holds on every unit member with `β₁(ER) ≥ 2`.** A new exact procedure decides zero-fibre asymptotes: two members are asymptotic iff no non-coincident overlap state reachable from a side branch of the central overlap chain lies on a cycle. The census covers 936 unit members with at least two periodic letters on both sides. (NM_V) holds on 888 and fails on 48 (24 left, 24 right), consistently with round-4 Lemma A. All 492 members with `β₁(ER) ≥ 2` satisfy it, and every failure has `β₁ ≤ 1`. An independent literal-word oracle confirms all 96 failing pairs. On the corpus, Theorems UP and UP′ therefore apply wherever odd-determinant rank two is not already excluded by B15 Theorem 22. Nothing new is excluded, since the corpus has no strict system. - **2026-11-18 — periodic partners after UP′: periodicity is available, zero-fibre anchoring and the contradiction are separate.** Barge–Diamond–Swanson §2 simultaneously periodicizes an asymptotic pair by one translation; finite-fibre lifting makes its common strict partner periodic too. At a branch normalized to zero, periodicity/zero-fibre membership is equivalent to persistent separation of the differing endpoint letters; on three letters Lean reduces this to their two-step images being unequal. If those letters merge, unshifted inflation collapses the two leaves, so compactness loses distinctness. Exact word/vector identities reconstruct the `1/012/21` (NM_V) failure. A connected `K₃,₂` finite partner model has repeated endpoint letters, degrees one and two, compatible co-partner equivalence, and no triangle; it is not a geometric strict-system realization. The `012/120/201` triangle remains a rank-at-least-three control. No C4 or PSC claim changes. - **2026-11-19 — the periodic-partner deductions are formalized conditionally; a fixed branch origin matters.** Lean now derives P/E/M, left/right transfer, exact-ray-to-metric asymptoticity, and their joint ternary classification from primitive local patch/endpoint laws and explicitly named BDS/R4 inputs. - **2026-11-19 — a cohomology bound on Barge's stack quotient excludes odd-determinant rank-two strict systems.** Directional witnesses and (W4) put the periodic seam images into at most two groups of endpoint letters on each side. The kernel has dimension three. On pullbacks from the quotient the evaluations factor through the grouped two-by-two cycle space of dimension at most one. The resulting dimension bound of four contradicts Barge's Theorem 22 on the quotient, whose dilation has cubic degree and odd norm and whose coincidence rank is two. - **2026-11-19 — ordinary local topology needs an open quotient, and its source metric must remain separate.** Fixed translations are homeomorphisms in the exact-agreement metric, but every nonempty translation orbit is discontinuous in time there. The chart `(t,T) -> T-t` gives a separate ordinary hull; its saturation by fixed fibre shifts proves it is an open quotient, and the explicit small-translation/window basis identifies the topology. Joint translation continuity descends through `id x chart`; the product of an arbitrary quotient would not suffice. Inflation descends through `(t,T) -> (beta*t,inflate T)`. For the concrete realization, flow equivariance yields an iff between ordinary-hull continuity and exact-source continuity, leaving both unproved without the remaining transverse argument. Bare bijectivity of inflation supplies no continuous inverse. No compactness, recognizability, finite-fibre or C4/PSC conclusion follows.
